**Status and button.** The row is combined from the same `SideCells.Decide` cells Advanced draws (`InstallSimpleRules.Decide`), over the sides Simple acts on, so the two views never disagree. First match wins:

| Sides Simple acts on | Status | Button (acts on) |
|---|---|---|
| A step running on a side | that step's words (`Downloading…`, `Installing on Forms Test…`), pale blue | its cancel, when it has one (that side) |
| A pending install on any side | `Loads at next start` (pale blue) | **Cancel install** (every side that offers it) |
| A pending removal on any side | `Removed at next start` (pale blue) | **Cancel uninstall** (every side that offers it) |
| A refused step | the side's own words (`Install failed`, `Server busy`) | **Install** when the side keeps a retry |
| Any side behind the listing or the other side | `↑ Update available` (amber) | **Update** (every side that offers it) |
| One side installed, another missing | `! Missing on server` / `! Missing on this PC` (amber) | **Install** (the missing side) |
| Every side installed | `✓ Installed` (green) | **Uninstall** (every side that can); a copy another tool manages reads `Managed by …` with no button |
| No side installed | `Not installed` (muted) | **Install** (every side that offers it) |

`✓`, `↑` and `!` are drawn only when the game font has them, as in the side cells. **One confirmation for both sides:** Install or Update on both runs the existing `Install on both` / `Update both` flow; its box names this PC and the server and has no extra "Yes, install on both" button. Uninstall, Cancel install and Cancel uninstall on both open the existing whole-row confirmation, which names every half it reaches. Restart notes per side are as in Advanced. A one-side button runs that side's Advanced button and its box.

**Readback and click keys** (`mods_ui widget_read` / `widget_click`):
- Toolbar: `view {mode: simple|advanced, stored, switchShown, actsOnServer}`; in Simple also `sort` (`released` or `name`) and `sortDirection`. Click `toolbar/view-simple` and `toolbar/view-advanced`.
- Filter row (`filters`, Simple): `filters {view, show: all|installed|update, counts {all, installed, update}, atDefaults, rows}`. Click `filters/show-all`, `filters/show-installed`, `filters/show-update`.
- Listing row (Simple only): `listing.simple {kind, status, statusText, glyph, tone, button, buttonName, targets [client, server], acted [...], listed, hidden}`. `kind` is `not-installed`, `installed`, `update`, `missing-server`, `missing-client`, `pending`, `removal-pending`, `busy`, `refused` or `managed`. Click the button with `<key>/install` and the chevron with `<key>/expand`.
- Header (Simple): `columns {view: simple, set [mod, released, downloads, version, status], keys, rowWidth, widths, dropped, dropPriority, sortable [name, released, downloads], sort, sortDirection}`. Click `header/name`, `header/released` or `header/downloads` to sort.

**What Simple acts on (SIMPLESCOPE1, project rule 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested in `InstallSimpleViewTests`, not live-verified).** Simple acts on this PC alone unless the connected server's route is open to this player. That means the remote route, which the server opens for an admin on its admin list or authenticated with `adminauth` (the status the client already receives), or the joined server is this PC's configured folder. A player who is not the server's admin sees no server-only listings. A both-sides listing acts on this PC's half, and its drawer adds a muted note: `The server half runs on <server>; its admins manage it. Simple installs and removes only this PC's copy.` The Show chips and counts use the same rows. Not connected: unchanged. Readback: `mods_ui` → `view.simpleScope` (`all` or `client-only`) and `view.simpleScopeReason` (`not connected to a server`, `not an admin on the connected server`, `an admin, but not allowed to install on the connected server`, `the connected server has remote installs switched off`, `the connected server doesn't run Progenitor`, `checking what the connected server allows`, or for `all`, `an admin on the connected server` / `the connected server is this PC's server folder`); each Simple row's `simple.serverHalfNote`. Every Install, Update or Uninstall box for a server or both-sides mod states each side. The `Server` and `Also on` facts name the sides it changes. A `Not changed` fact names the side it leaves alone: `This client: its copy stays as it is` or `This client: nothing is installed here` on a Server-cell action, and on a this-PC-only action, for the server's admin, `<server>: its copy stays as it is` when the server has a copy, `<server> (it doesn't have this mod)` when it has none, or `<server>: nothing changes there` while nothing has said; for a player who is not its admin, `<server>: its admins manage the server half`. This holds in Advanced too. The server-copy reading is built and game-free tested in `InstallSimpleViewTests`, not live-verified.

### Simple view acceptance procedure (not run)

Authorized test session, test client, **Install mods** open; read with `mods_ui widget_read`, screenshot each step.
1. With `[Mods] InstallView` absent: the switch reads **Simple** selected; no Client/Server columns, picker, Side or Only groups; chips `All N`, `Installed N`, `Updates N`; no tool (r2modman), library (ExampleCommandFramework) or server-only row.
2. Not connected: a both-sided mod's `listing.simple.targets` is `[client]`; Install opens the client-only box.
3. Connected as a non-admin: the same, and server-only mods are not listed.
4. Connected as the server's admin: a both-sided mod reads `targets [client, server]`; Install opens one box naming this PC and the server; server-only mods are listed with `targets [server]`.
5. `widget_click toolbar/view-advanced`: today's screen; restart the game: Advanced is still selected. Switch back with `toolbar/view-simple`.

## Restart for pending installs (INSTALLRESTART1)

**Status: source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested (`InstallRestartTests`, plus the password case in `JoinFlowStage2ClientTests`); not live-verified.** No Bootstrap change: Core and Dev hot-swap. Live recipe (not run): [bridge-ops-ui.md](bridge-ops-ui.md#restart-for-pending-installs-recipe-installrestart1).

A client install, update or removal that loads only at the next start used to leave the player with `Loads at next start` and nothing to press. Now the Install tab (Simple and Advanced) draws a **pending-restart bar** above its column header, and the Mods tab draws the same bar at the top of its list, while any such change waits:

| Where the player is | Bar | Confirmation (dialog kit) |
|---|---|---|
| Not in a world | `1 mod loads after a restart` · **Restart now** | `Restart V Rising to finish installing 1 mod?` · `V Rising closes and starts again.` |
| In a world on a dedicated server this client can dial again (`SteamIPv4` or `Lidgren`) | `2 mods load after a restart` · **Restart and rejoin <server>** | `Restart V Rising to finish installing 2 mods?` · `You'll rejoin <server> automatically.` |
| In a private game this client hosts (Bootstrap `IsHostingPrivateGame`) | `… load after a restart` · **Restart and resume <save>** | `You'll return to <save> automatically: V Rising continues your private game.` |
| In a world nothing can bring the player back to (a hosted game whose save name is unknown, a Steam P2P or EOS join) | `… load after a restart · you'll return to the menu` · **Restart now** | `You'll return to the main menu: <why>` |

- **Counted.** Rows whose client half is pending (`Loads at next start`), a client copy waiting while the local server already loads its own, and pending removals with a client half (then the bar says `N mod changes apply after a restart` and the box names both kinds: `Restart V Rising to finish 1 install and 1 removal?`, or `… to finish removing 1 mod?`; F29, live 2026-10-08). Changes waiting only on a server are not counted, because a game restart does not apply them; the bottom pending bar and **Stop server to apply** are unchanged. Before the Install tab's index is loaded (the Mods tab opened first), the client ledger's pending downloads are counted. **F28 (live 2026-10-08; built and game-free tested in `InstallRestartTests`, not live-verified):** every client ledger row whose file carries its removal marker (the Mods page's Uninstall of a loaded copy, marked for the next start) also counts, once per package and not when the list already names that mod. Before, a removal of a mod the Install tab's index does not list reached neither the Install tab's **Restart and rejoin** nor the Mods tab's bar (log `restart … →none`), and `mods_ui restart press` did nothing.
- **Look.** The bar is a solid band (`CellRowSpec.Band`, the mod page's status band) with the sentence in the pending tone and the settings rows' key-cap button (`CellSkin.KeyCap`). In the Mods tab's narrow column it is two band rows: the sentence, then the button.
- **Buttons.** **Restart** / **Not now**. When the relauncher cannot run here (Steam not running, no `steam://` handler, no Windows PowerShell, or no relauncher at all), the box says so: title `V Rising can't restart itself here`, body `<why>. Quit to desktop, then start V Rising from Steam. Start it again within 10 minutes and Progenitor will rejoin <server>.`, and **Quit to desktop** / **Not now**.

**What Restart does** (`JoinFlowClient.RunRestart`):
1. Writes `BepInEx/config/progenitor-install-restart.json`: the changes (name, plugin id when known, version, `install` / `update` / `remove`) and where the restart returns. No address, no password.
2. In a world, writes JOINFLOW2's rejoin record `progenitor-rejoin.json` ([join-flow.md](join-flow.md#7-restart-and-rejoin-stage-2)). For a server: its address, port, transport, name and whether it used a password (never the password). For a hosted game: `kind hosted` and `save`.
3. In a world, leaves the game's own way (the `ExitGame` view, as the bridge's `disconnect`), so the server saves the character, and waits up to 30 s for the world to go.
4. Starts JOINFLOW2's relauncher (the same detached PowerShell command through `steam://run/1604030`) and quits with `Application.Quit`. On the manual path it quits without a relauncher. One restart per process (`already restarting`).

**After the restart.** Bootstrap promotes the pending files at start as always, so the Install rows read Installed and the bar is gone. At the title screen, Core reads the install-restart record once (at most 10 minutes old), then:
- **Nothing to return to:** a notice, `Restart finished` · `Installed: ChatSkin.` (also `Removed: …`, and `Not loaded: …` for a change this start does not show).
- **A server:** the **Rejoining <server>** page connects as JOINFLOW2's rejoin does and, once joined, reads `Installed: ChatSkin. Rejoined <server>.` A server joined with a password is dialed through the game's own password prompt (`GameHelper.LaunchPasswordProtectedGame`), which the player or a mod such as RetainedPassword fills; Progenitor never stores it. JOINPW1 (another branch) makes the Server tab's Join connect without a password first and prompt only on `IncorrectPassword`; this rejoin uses the hint recorded seconds before the quit, so it prompts at once.
- **A hosted game:** the **Resuming <save>** page waits for the title screen's Continue to name the save (`MainMenuNewView._ContinueText`, 10 s grace), then presses the game's own Continue (`MainMenuNewView.ContinueButton_OnClick`, which hosts the newest save with its own settings through `HostGame`). Observed 2026-10-06: Continue resumes a hosted private game (traced in the internal research notes). Once the world is up the page reads `Installed: ChatSkin. Resumed <save>.` A Continue for another save or a newer server is refused: `The title screen's Continue is for X, not Y. Start Y from Play.`

**Readback and bridge.**
- `mods_ui widget_read` on the band (row `install-restart`, cells `restart-text` and `restart-button`; Mods tab rows `mods-restart` and `mods-restart-button`) carries `pendingRestart {count, mods [{id, name, version, action}], kind, shown, canRestart, manual, manualWhy, sentence, button, confirm {title, body, yes, no}, rejoinTarget}`. `rejoinTarget` is `{kind none, why}`, `{kind dedicated, name, address, port, protocol, needsPassword}` or `{kind hosted, save}`.
- `mods_ui restart` works with the workspace closed. `op`: `read` (default), `press` (the button; opens the confirmation), `confirm` (the real restart; with `dryRun true` it writes both records marked `dryRun`, reports the relaunch command, and neither leaves nor quits), `cancel` (Not now), `pickup` (takes a dry run's install-restart record now; the rejoin record's dry run is picked up by `join_ui rejoin_now`).
- The answer adds `lastRestart {dryRun, offer, mode, why, fileName, arguments, leaveFirst, recordPath, rejoinRecordPath}`, `installRecord`, `rejoinRecord` (with `kind` and `save`), `resume {save, phase, error, continueSeen, pressed}`, `lastPickup` and `afterRestart`. Log lines start `Install restart:`; the return logs `JoinFlow:`.

**Not live-verified:** all of it. In particular: the leave-then-quit order, the relauncher under Steam (unproven in JOINFLOW2 too), `_ContinueText` being filled when the title view first reports ready, a title-screen popup blocking Continue, and the hosted server's catalog name matching the save's Continue caption.

## Install tab in the game's idiom (VANILLA1)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client 3840×1080 in game on the isolated test server, branch ui/install-vanilla-sweep 530e6fc1 rebased on main 33b45da9):** small-caps headers over the column-tinted divider, vanilla popup-button chips with the warm selected plate, faint row separators, framed empty icon slot (HotDemo), muted Downloads and listing count; side cells at the 18% tint read as a dark wash with the full-strength 3 px left bar and colored buttons; the hovered row (Almanacs) showed a slight cool lift with "No server side" still readable. The first round's 40% tint (installsides-38-sweep.png) read as solid blocks, which the linear-blend model explains. **Not live-verified:** the 1600×1024 capture and chip hover sprite swaps.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Tests: `InstallVanillaSweepTests` (palette, typography, chip fit, no overlap, runtime pins), plus the updated pins in `InstallSidesPaneTests`, `DialogsVanillaTests` and `InstallColumnsTests`.

The request was for the Install tab to "feel first class / part of the Vanilla program". The approved structure stays: the per-side Client and Server columns with their blue and red identity, the filter groups, the chevron drawer and the server picker. The references are the game's measured settings list, popup and menu tabs (details in the internal research notes). A list entry there is never boxed. A hovered entry gets a faint hue wash with a light top gradient (`ListButton_Glow2` + `ListButton_TopGradient`). A modified entry carries a 6 px bar at its left. Entries are split by a faint steel line, section headers sit on `Divider02_LeftToRight`, value boxes are `SimpleBox_Normal`, and buttons are the 9-sliced `SmallerButton_*` / `SimpleMenuButton_*`. Menu tabs draw mixed-case words in small caps, and secondary text uses the resting gray (0.906 x 0.7).

### Critique (before) and what borrows from the game

| Gap in the 2026-10-03 capture | Vanilla element borrowed | Change |
|---|---|---|
| The side columns are opaque, saturated blue and red rectangles with a 1 px Unity outline: a spreadsheet, not a game list | The settings entry's hue wash and top gradient; the `Modified` edge bar | A translucent wash of the column's hue (18% alpha since round 2; see below), no rim, the borrowed `ListButton_TopGradient` tinted to the column along the top, and a 3 px edge bar in the column's accent at the left (`MenuPalette.ColumnLook`) |
| Header bands are filled boxes with a top rule | The section header's `Divider02_LeftToRight` under its title | No fill. The caption sits over the game's divider sprite, tinted to the column (kit 2 px line when not borrowed) |
| Grayed cells ("No client side") are near-black boxes that still look like content | The game shows nothing where nothing applies | No wash; only the edge bar at 22% alpha keeps the column's line. The words stay faint |
| Header captions are tiny all-caps (`MOD`, `RELEASED ▼`) | The menu tabs' mixed-case small caps | Own case in small caps at 15 px (`CellSpec.SmallCaps`, `InstallColumns.HeaderFontSize`). Widths are measured with the small-caps rule, so no caption is cut. Other kit headers keep their upper case |
| Filter chips are flat gold and brown rectangles with outlines | The popup button (`SmallerButton_*`) with its hover and pressed swaps | `CellSkin.Chip`: the game sprite. Selected adds a warm plate inside the sprite and a bright caption; unselected keeps the bare sprite and the resting-gray caption; attention adds an amber caption. Falls back to the kit chip when the art is not ready |
| The search box is a flat field with a 1 px outline | The settings value box (`SimpleBox_Normal`, `SimpleBox_Hover`) | `CellSkin.ValueBox`. The kit outline turns off only when the sprite drew |
| Rows float on the backdrop with nothing between them | The faint steel line between settings entries | `CellRowSpec.Separator`: a 1 px line in the entry gradient's steel at 16% alpha on every listing row and on the pending bar. Never on a joined drawer row |
| Row hover is the kit's near-black, and the selected row has a solid fill | The Mods list's soft row, already the workspace's list idiom | Listing rows and the drawer use the `soft` selected fill (`InstallModsPane.ListRowsSoft`). Round 2: the hover is the game's entry hue at 5% (`MenuPalette.RowHoverQuiet`, `CellRowSpec.QuietHover`) |
| Downloads are bright white and outshine the mod name; the count is bright too | Secondary text in the resting gray | Downloads and the listing count are muted, like Released |
| A missing icon is an opaque black block | Quiet framed slots | `CellSkin.IconWell`: a faint dark fill with a steel rim |
| Side buttons are the game sprite with the column plate | Same | Kept (user-approved hue, live review 3). The plate helper is now shared with the chips (`SetPlate`) |
| Pending bar | The key-cap button is already the game's | Kept, plus the separator line above it |
| Empty state ("No matching listings.") and loading text are plain labels | The game centers quiet empty-state text | **Not changed.** It is a kit label, and moving it into a cell row is a separate change |

**Width limit, unchanged by this sweep:** a listing row needs about 1010 px (expand, icon, the Mod floor and two fixed 330 px side columns), which dates from INSTALLSIDES2. The full-width pane never gives less: the live row is 1236 px at both 3840x1080 and 1600x1024. The toolbar and filter rows fit at 860 px. A test pins this limit, so it cannot silently become an overlap.

New palette tokens, all additions: `ColumnClientWash`, `ColumnServerWash`, `PaneUnder`, `ColumnEdgeWidth`, `ColumnTopAlpha`, `ColumnGrayEdgeAlpha`, `RowSeparator`, `ChipOnPlate`, `ChipRestText`, `IconWellFill` and `IconWellRim`. No existing token changed. The INSTALLSIDES2 opaque-band tokens stay defined but no longer draw.

### Round 2: quieter cells and hover (decided on the first live capture)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client, Core 2026.10.39.1, capture `installsides-38-sweep`):** the headers, small caps, dividers, chips, row lines, framed missing icon and muted secondary text looked right. The next request was to "go quieter on the cells and fix the hover". The 40% washes still read as solid blocks: client (31, 66, 102), server (91, 22, 27). On a hovered row, the soft hover lifted the list to (67, 58, 54). That washed out the bands and sank the grayed "No server side" text to about 1.4:1.

**Cause:** the game blends in linear color space, so a translucent layer over the near-black pane looks much stronger than an sRGB estimate. The 40% client wash was predicted at #202a40 and read #1f4266. `MenuPalette.OverLinear` models the game's blend. A test checks it against the three captured colors within 4 levels.

**Change (source-checked, built and game-free tested; not live-verified):**
- **Wash:** 40% to 18% for both columns (`ColumnClientWash` #2f6aa0, `ColumnServerWash` #8e2a30). Over the pane (#160000) they composite to about (28, 45, 71) and (66, 13, 16). 18% rather than 20–25% because at 20% the muted words fall to 4.41:1 on the client wash under hover.
- **Kept:** the 3 px edge bar at full strength in the column accent (#5ea2dc / #d0585e) and the tinted side buttons, which carry the identity.
- **Top gradient:** 30% to 20% (`ColumnTopAlpha`).
- **Hover:** listing rows use `RowHoverQuiet`, the game's settings-entry hover hue (0.163, 0.395, 0.585) at 5%. It lifts the bare list to about (23, 19, 33), a cool lift rather than a gray one. The selected (drawer-open) fill is unchanged. The Mods tab's own soft hover is unchanged.
- **Measured WCAG contrast (linear model):**
  - Muted, normal, installed green, amber and pending blue words on the client and server washes, at rest and hovered: at least 4.55:1. The worst case is muted on the client wash under hover.
  - The same words on the bare list, plus the Mod column's warn note, at rest and hovered: at least 5.29:1.
  - Grayed words: 2.59:1 at rest, 2.32:1 hovered (the soft hover gave about 1.4:1).

## Download limit, install on both, typing (LIMITS1)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client 3840x1080 in game on the isolated test server, branch ui/install-limits-both-perf 8d0473be):** r2modman read "Too large to install (346 MB; limit 128 MB)"; clicking a plain BepInEx plugin package's Client Install showed "Yes, install on both" on its own row above [No] [Yes, client only] (answered No); bridge `mods_ui search` re-filters with a warm cache took about 1 ms when few rows changed, 33–40 ms when about 180 of 301 rows were shown or hidden (logged "Install: re-filter took … ms"), and 241 ms once on a cold cache (`typing.maxMs`). **Not live-verified:** real keyboard typing echo in the search field (the bridge applies search immediately, without the debounce), the both-sides install itself, and `MaxDownloadMB` overrides. A re-filter that shows or hides most rows still exceeds the 16 ms budget once per pause in typing.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Tests: `InstallNonModTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests` (the both offer), `InstallListPolishTests` (typing).

**Download limit.** `[Install] MaxDownloadMB` (default **128**, read clamped to 8–1024, MiB) is the package download ceiling: the listing-time "Too large to install (… ; limit 128 MB)" rule and the fetch-time ceiling of a package download both read it (`NonModPackages.DownloadLimit`; `InstallCatalogService` sets its fetch options from it unless a caller passed its own cap). The old 32 MiB value was copied on 2026-09-13 from the server-to-client mod transfer cap (`[Networking] ModTransferMaxBytes`, removed 2026-10-05 with the transfer), which existed because transfers over the game connection run at 512 KiB/s one at a time; a plain HTTPS download only needs a safety bound. The package index keeps its own 32 MiB bound (`CatalogFetcher.IndexMaxBytes`, for the index fetch and the menu's index refresh; the JSON parser has its own 8 MiB ceiling). In today's index the largest real mods are about 25 MB (`Tovan-BossRosterConfig`) and 22 MB (`PlainSky-VRenderPerfMod`); `ebkr-r2modman` (346 MB) stays blocked (and is a mod-manager app); `SampleMods-SampleRconClient` (118 MB) is tagged `Tools` and stays a tool.

**Install on both from one side.** Install or Update pressed on one side opens the usual confirmation; when the row's own `Install on both` / `Update both` shortcut would be offered (both side cells carry the same action, so both routes allow it), the box offers both: the main button reads **Yes, client only** / **Yes, server only** and a second yes, **Yes, install on both** / **Yes, update both**, closes this box and runs the existing both-sides flow (the shortcut's). The three captions do not fit one row of the 640-wide popup at full width, so the both button is a full-width game popup button in its own row above **No** and **Yes, … only** (`DialogContent.ExtrasStacked`; nothing is ellipsized). Enter answers with the side-only yes. The client cell's box that offers both names each side and which answer installs it, the way the Server cell's box names its server: `Server` `<name> (only with Yes, install on both)`, its `Folder` (local route) or `Address` (remote route), `Server copy` `Now if it hot-loads, else at the next server restart`, and `This client` with this client's effect followed by `(both answers)` (for example `At the next game restart (both answers)`); it has no `Not changed` or `Takes effect` row. The Server cell's box that offers both names this client the same way, `This client` `Only with Yes, install on both`, instead of a `Not changed` row. Built and game-free tested in `InstallListViewTests`, not live-verified. A mod both sides need (manifest-verified) whose other side cannot be acted on gets one yes and a note why: no server picked, the private games load this client's copy, only a server admin can install it, or the route's own note.

**Typing in Search mods.** Live (client log, Core 2026.10.28.x): each keystroke cost 114–191 ms in the input handler (`pumpMs` of the `ui hitch` lines), mostly re-deciding both side cells of every listing for the Show counts. The separate 600–770 ms hitches in that log follow full Install pane rebuilds (`hide`/`show 'Install mods'` after a click), not keystrokes. Now:
- a keystroke only records the text (the field echoes it itself) and schedules one re-filter 220 ms after the last keystroke (`SearchDebounceSeconds`); each keystroke supersedes the last; the bridge's `mods_ui search` still applies at once;
- the re-filter shows or hides the existing rows in place (no rebuild) and touches only rows whose visibility changes;
- the per-side decisions for the Show filter and its counts are cached across re-filters and dropped on a build, a new install scan, a row's install action, a remote-install update or a picker change;
- the toolbar readback carries `typing {refilters, lastMs, maxMs, lastChangedRows, countsMs, cachedDecisions, overBudget, budgetMs (16), pending, debounceMs}`, and a re-filter over 16 ms logs `Install: re-filter took X ms (N rows shown or hidden of M; budget 16 ms)`. `lastMs` is this pane's own work (visibility writes, counts, the one batched layout); Unity's canvas rebuild after it shows in the `ui hitch summary` (file-only, every 30 s; see bridge.md, "Logging policy").
Game-free the fake runtime has no Unity layout (a re-filter of 150 rows measures about 0.2 ms there), so the live numbers are the ones to read.

### Acceptance (not run)

1. `[Install] MaxDownloadMB` absent: a 25 MB mod installs; r2modman still reads too large (346 MB; limit 128 MB). Set it to 16: the 25 MB mod reads too large. The index still loads.
2. A both-tagged mod with a local server: client Install opens [Yes, install on both] above [No] [Yes, client only]; the both button installs both halves. With picker None: one yes; for a manifest-verified both-sided mod the note says no server is picked.
3. Type "blood" quickly: letters echo without lag; one `typing.refilters` increment after the pause; `typing.lastMs` stays under ~16 ms per frame while typing, and the next `ui hitch summary` names no Install handler in `top`.

## Listings that are not installable mods (FIX-NONMODS)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client in game on the isolated test server, branch fix/install-nonmods f9e9ef61 rebased on main cc26c57a):** ebkr-r2modman read "Too large to install (346 MB; limit 32 MB)" on both sides with no button; SampleMods-SampleRconClient read "Too large to install (118 MB; limit 32 MB)" with no button; the install confirm dialog's fact label read "Mod" in full (Almanacs 2.42.95, canceled with No). **Not live-verified:** the mod-manager wording on a manager that fits the size limit (none in today's index), the drawer explanation, and the filter-count exclusions.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Live bug (2026-10-03): Install on `ebkr-r2modman` 3.2.20 (a mod manager application; categories `Gloomrot Update`, `1.0 Update`, so not a tool by tag; `file_size` 363,105,843) opened the confirmation and downloaded up to the 32 MiB ceiling before failing with "the download exceeded the size ceiling". Tests: `InstallNonModTests`, `DialogTests.A_short_fact_label_is_never_cut`.

- **Too large.** A listing whose published `file_size` exceeds the download limit (`CatalogFetcher.DefaultMaxBytes`, 32 MiB) is never offered: both side cells read `Too large to install (346 MB; limit 32 MB)` (binary MB) muted with no button, the drawer's warning line says why, and `InstallCatalogService.DownloadAndInspectAsync` refuses it before any request (`size ceiling (…)`); the fetcher's own ceiling stays as the backstop for a listing that under-reports its size. In the client's cached index today this is `ebkr-r2modman` and `SampleMods-SampleRconClient` (118 MB).
- **Mod manager apps.** `NonModPackages.KnownModManagers` lists them by Thunderstore full name (owner-name), never a display name: `ebkr-r2modman` (the only listing in V Rising's cached index whose description calls it a mod manager, checked 2026-10-03) and `Kesomannen-GaleModManager` (not in the index today). Such a row reads `Mod manager app, not a game mod` with no button and the drawer explains; the catalog refuses it before any request.
- Both kinds are handled as tools (`InstallModsPane.IsTool` now also answers them; the Thunderstore `Tools` tag is `IsToolCategory`): never installable, no shortcut, not counted by the Show filter's Installed / Missing a side / Needs update, not in the pending count.
- **Fact labels in dialogs.** The confirmation's `Mod` label drew `M…`: the label column was the glyph estimate alone, while the text cell draws inside its padding. The column now adds the cell's padding (`DialogLayout.FactLabelPad`) and never goes under 56 px (`FactLabelMinWidth`). This is the likely cause from the code; the live label is not re-checked.

### Acceptance (not run)

1. Install tab: `r2modman` reads `Too large to install (346 MB; limit 32 MB)` on both sides, no button; its drawer explains; the client log shows no download for it.
2. `SampleRconClient` the same (118 MB).
3. Show → Installed / Missing a side / Needs update never include them.
4. Any install confirmation: the `Mod` fact label reads in full.

## Install filter bar (INSTALLFILTERS1)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client 3840x1080 in game on the isolated test server, Core 2026.10.31.1, branch ui/install-filters):** two filter rows (Side + Show, Only + summary + Clear filters) under the search/picker row, selected chips filled gold; counts All 151, Installed 5, Missing a side 0, Needs update 0; Show Installed listed exactly the 5 mods with a copy on either side ("Showing 5 · installed · current game update only"); after a hot client uninstall of HotDemo, Missing a side read 1 and filtered to HotDemo alone ("! Missing: the server has it"), then HotDemo was hot-reinstalled. **Not live-verified:** Needs update with a real outdated install, the amber attention rim, picker None/Private games dropping Missing to 0, the narrow-window wrap, and Clear filters.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Requested 2026-10-03: "we need to make the filters up top readable, and add a filter for missing (aka one side has it but not the other) and needs update." Tests: `InstallFilterBlockTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests` (predicates, counts, readback), `InstallListViewTests`, `InstallListPolishTests`, `InstallSearchFilterTests`.

**Groups.** Three labeled groups on the kit's chip buttons:

| Group | Chips | Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| **Side** | Any · Client · Server · Both | Single choice (unchanged meaning). |
| **Show** | All · Installed · Missing a side · Needs update, each with its count (`Missing a side 2`) | Single choice (`InstallSearchFilters.Show`). |
| **Only** | No restart needed · Current game update (Oakveil) | Independent toggles (unchanged meaning; the second was "Current update"). |

Beside the Only group a one-line summary says what the list shows: `Showing 12 · missing a side · client mods · current game update only` (the count shown, then each filter that narrows the list, and the search). **Clear filters** (off the defaults only) resets Side, Show and Only and the search. Filters persist for the session per catalog, like before; the search does not: it is emptied when the workspace closes and is never remembered ([SEARCHCLEAR1](#clearing-a-search-searchclear1)).

**Look.** Chip tones (new tokens, no existing token changed): selected — face `#6b4f22`, rim `#e0b25a`, caption `#fff3dc`; unselected — face `#211a14`, rim `#4a3c2f`, caption `#cbbda7`; the two attention chips (Missing a side, Needs update) keep the dark face with an amber rim `#d49a3a` while their count is above zero. They are the kit's flat chip buttons (as before), not a borrowed game sprite.

**Predicates** (`InstallModsPane.ShowPredicate`, over the same `SideCells.Decide` decision each row draws, so a chip and the rows cannot disagree):
- **Installed** — either side's cell holds a copy: `installed`, `update`, `older`, `pending` or `removal-pending`.
- **Missing a side** — either side's cell is `missing` (the row's `! Missing: the server has it`): a manifest-verified both-sided mod one side has and the other lacks, for the picked server. A side the mod does not use (`No client side` / `No server side`), picker **None** (`No server selected`) and **Private games** (`Same as client` / `Not loaded there`) are never missing; Thunderstore tags alone never make a missing side.
- **Needs update** — either side's cell is `update` (behind the listing) or `older` (behind the other side of a both-sided mod) — the row's `↑`.

**Counts** are how many listings each Show choice would show with the search, the Side group and the Only toggles as they are (the Show choice itself aside). They are recomputed whenever the filter rows are built or synced: a search, a filter click, an install or uninstall, a picker change or an index refresh (each rebuilds or re-filters the list).

**Rows and widths.** The filter bar is two rows under the search and picker row: `filters` (Side and Show) and `filters-only` (Only, the summary, Clear filters). On a row too narrow for Side and Show side by side (below about 1180 px) the Show group wraps whole to its own row, `filters-show`, between them; groups never split and never share the search row. This adds one pinned row compared with the old single chip row.

**Readback and keys.** Each filter row's readback carries `filters {side, show (all|installed|missing|update), counts {all, installed, missing, update}, summary, rows [...], noRestart, currentUpdate, currentUpdateTag, currentUpdateSource, atDefaults}`. Clicks: `filters/side-any|side-client|side-server|side-both`, `filters/show-all|show-installed|show-missing|show-update` (`filters-show/…` when wrapped), `filters-only/norestart|current|clear`. Note the Only toggles and Clear moved from `filters/…` to `filters-only/…`.

### Filter bar acceptance procedure (not run)

Authorized session, test client, **Install mods** open; `mods_ui widget_read` / `rects`, screenshot each step.
1. At 3840×1080: two filter rows under the search/picker row, labeled Side / Show / Only; Any, All and Current game update read as filled gold, the rest dark; nothing overlaps or is cut.
2. With HotDemo missing on the client (the server has it): `Missing a side 1` with an amber rim; click it — only HotDemo shows, the summary reads `Showing 1 · missing a side · current game update only`.
3. With an outdated install: `Needs update N` amber-rimmed; click — only those rows (each with `↑`).
4. Installed: the count matches the rows with a copy on either side.
5. Change the picker to None and Private games: `Missing a side` drops to 0 for mods only the server had.
6. Combine with Side = Client and No restart needed: counts and summary follow; Clear filters resets all.
7. At 1600×1024: still two rows, nothing cut; at a narrower window the Show group on its own row (`filters-show`).

## Install rows per side, round 2 (INSTALLSIDES2)

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release), game-free tested, and partly live-verified** (2026-10-03, Steam client 3840×1080 in game on the isolated test server, Core 2026.10.28.3 to .5). Seen live: no Side column; blue Client and red Server bands and headers; side buttons on the vanilla sprite with the solid hue plate reading blue and red; grayed cells for a side the mod does not use receding (then worded `Not used by this mod`, now `No client side` / `No server side`); `✓` (font check logged `drawn`) and `!` for a missing half; the compact drawer with no inner scroll and the per-side restart note; `Read full description` opening the reader (vanilla popup, headings, one scroll bar, Close); the list staying still on expand, switch and collapse (BoneDrops's row kept its y on open, HotDemo's kept its y when switching from BoneDrops and on collapse); HotDemo's Client cell no longer reading `Installed · Managed elsewhere`; the staging sweep (`removed 176 abandoned download(s), 62.9 MB`). **Not live-verified:** the admin lock (the connected server resolves to this PC's folder, which needs no admin), hover and pressed looks of the tinted buttons, the facts grid at other widths, and the column widths below 1624 px. User-approved after the live review of INSTALLSIDES1 (2026-10-03). Where this disagrees with the INSTALLSIDES1 section below, this one is current. Tests: `SideCellsTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests`, `InstallListViewTests`, `InstallColumnsTests`, `PanelLayoutTests`, `StagingTempSweepTests`.

**Column colors.** The Client and Server columns each carry a tint through the header, the cells and the buttons (`CellSpec.Column`). A row draws one band behind each column's cells (a flat fill with the kit's one-pixel rim); the header draws the column's band with a 2 px top rule in its accent, and its caption in the column's light text. The side buttons stay the game's `SmallerButton_*` popup sprites (normal, hover, pressed and disabled swap as before): the sprite's color is multiplied by a nearly white column hue (so its light rim and its hover / pressed swaps keep their brightness), a solid plate in a color brighter than the band is laid over it at 55% alpha under the caption, inset 8 px from the ends and 3 px from the top and bottom so the sprite's decorated ends and rim (and their hover / pressed swaps) show around it (`column-tint`), and the caption takes the column's light text. Live review 3 measured the faces as drawn (client `#14203a`, server `#1a1418`): `SmallerButton_Normal`'s face is mostly transparent, so the row's band showed through it, and the earlier overlay — drawn with the same sprite — inherited that transparency and barely showed. Over those faces the plate is about `#34649e` / `#8b353a`. A column button's readback now lists the images it draws (`images`: name, sprite, color, alpha, enabled, active, sibling index, material) so the next live check can read the plate directly. Live review 2 found the 55%, darker overlay still read near-black. New palette tokens only (no existing token changed).

Live review 1 (Core 2026.10.28.3, 3840×1080): the mockup's bands (`#121c24` / `#221212`) read as near-black over the game's red backdrop, the multiply-only server buttons looked black, and the grayed cells came out lighter than the active ones. The values now:

| Token | Client (blue) | Server (red) |
|---|---|---|
| Cell band fill / rim | `#173350` / `#3f72a0` | `#461519` / `#8e3a40` |
| Header fill / top rule | `#1e4466` / `#5ea2dc` | `#5c1c22` / `#d0585e` |
| Button and header caption | `#d8ecff` | `#ffd8d8` |
| Sprite multiplier | `#e6f0fa` | `#fae6e6` |
| Plate over the sprite (alpha 0.55; 0.22 when disabled) | `#4f9cf0` | `#e8505a` |

Every word color a side cell uses (muted `#a3959a`, text, green, pale blue, amber) is at least 4.5:1 against both bands by the WCAG 2 formula (sRGB linearized; muted on the client band is the tightest, 4.5:1); the kit's own `ContrastRatio` uses unlinearized values and reads dark pairs lower. Tested in `InstallSidesPaneTests`; the look is not re-verified live.

**State colors and glyphs.** The words carry the state: light green installed (`OkText`), amber an update or a missing half (`PendText`), pale blue waiting on a restart — pending install or pending removal (new `InfoText` `#9fc7e8`) — and muted not installed. An installed side is preceded by `✓` (U+2713), an update by `↑` (U+2191, WGL4), and a missing half by `!` (live review 2: `↑` read as an update on `Missing: the server has it`), all in the state's color (amber for update and missing). The `!` is chosen in the pane (`InstallModsPane.SideGlyph`); `SideCell.Glyph` in the policy still answers `↑` for a missing half. U+2713 is outside WGL4, so the runtime asks the game's TMP font (with its fallbacks) for every glyph before drawing it (`TMP_FontAsset.HasCharacter`) and draws the words alone when the font lacks it; the answer is logged once per font and glyph (`Ui: glyph U+2713 in font '<name>': drawn` / `missing, words only`). Which of the two the vanilla font has is **not known yet**; the log line answers it on the first live build.

**Grayed sides.** A side the mod does not run on is a grayed cell that recedes — a band darker than the active ones, close to the list's backdrop (`#0c0809` at 55%), no rim, and its words dimmer than muted (`#5a5048`, `CellTone.Faint`) — reading **No client side** in the Client column and **No server side** in the Server column (approved wording 2026-10-03; was `Not used by this mod`, before that `Not needed`), with no button. With the picker on **None** every Server cell is grayed and reads **No server selected**. No diagonal hatch: there is no vanilla hatch sprite in the borrowed set, and a texture made in-kit would be an unmanaged object every Core generation must destroy; the flat dim fill is the documented fallback. The **Side** column is gone; the side filter chips still filter by side. **Widths (live review 1):** the Mod column had a large empty gap and `Removal pending server restart` wrapped, so each side column is 330 px at the 1236 px row and grows by half of every extra pixel of row width, up to 440 px (`InstallColumns.SideColumnWidthFor`): at the 1624 px row (3840×1080 live) the side columns are 440 px and Mod 404 px; at 1236 px they are 330 px and Mod 236 px. Released drops below a 1150 px row, Downloads below 1044 px.

**Not an admin.** When the route refuses the player for want of admin (`ServerRouteBlock.NotAdmin`), the Server cell is not grayed and keeps the words an admin would see (`Not installed`, `0.9.0 · update 1.0.0`, `Unknown`…), without the old `· Ask a server admin` note. The buttons an admin would get become one dim, disabled **Admin only**; a lock (U+1F512) is asked for before it, but it lies outside the font's plane, so in practice the caption is the words alone. When the existing admin-auth offer applies (`AdminAuthFlow.Offer`: on the server's admin list, not authenticated, `[Ui] AdminAuthButton` on) the button is **Authenticate as admin**, which runs the existing flow (the game's `adminauth`); while it runs it reads **Authenticating…** and is disabled. Remote installs authorize by list membership, so on today's servers a refused player is not on the list and sees **Admin only**. The drawer keeps the route's admin-list sentence. **Not seen live:** in the live review the connected server resolved to this PC's own folder (the local route, which needs no admin), so neither lock appeared; this part is game-free tested only.

**Drawer (option A).** The drawer is a compact, fixed panel with no scrolling of its own: a short description (the one-liner, then the README's opening paragraphs, each clamped to about 300 characters at a word, with `…`) and the facts as a two-column grid: the facts that fit half the body's text width (estimated at the body size, less a 24 px gutter) fill it in order, two to a line, the second column starting at 50%; a fact too long for half spans the whole line, after the grid (live review 1: pairing by character count left `Side` alone and misaligned the right column). The body is as tall as that text, at most 280 px, and always leaves 40 px of the next listing in view (`InstallDrawerLayout.CompactBodyHeight`). It registers no inner scroll, so the mouse wheel always scrolls the mod list. **Read full description** (now the game's popup button) opens the whole README in its own reader window: the CommonPopup-styled dialog with the listing's name as title, one scrolling text block (the kit's scroll text with the game's `ScrollBar_Bar` / `ScrollBar_Handle` sprites, headings bold and larger as before, at most 560 px) and **Close**; Escape closes it. Kept: the chevron is the only toggle, **Collapse**, the rail, fill and seam, one drawer at a time, the list kept still on a toggle (see below).

**The list stays still (feedback 2026-10-03: "the collapse and expansion of the drawers moves the whole window around, it should stay where it is, let the user scroll down to read it if it expands off screen").** Expanding, collapsing (the arrow or Collapse) and switching the open drawer no longer scroll the list. The clicked row is measured before the rebuild (its top from the list viewport's top edge) and, once the rebuild's rows are placed, the list's offset is moved by the difference so the row is back where it was (`UiReveal.TryAnchor`, `MenuPanelLayout.AnchorOffset`): opening row B while drawer A above it closes is compensated, so B does not jump. The offset is clamped only to what the content allows; a drawer that extends below the viewport stays there for the player to scroll to. A rebuild nobody clicked writes nothing. Readback: `drawer:<key>.anchoredTo {key, rowY, wantedY, offset}` (replaces `scrolledTo`).

**Per-side restart note.** The drawer head's note names each side the mod uses (`SideCells.RestartNote`): `Client: loads without a restart` / `Client: loads at the next game restart` (from the open plan when there is one, else the listing's own no-restart declaration), `Server: loads at the next server restart` (the local-folder route; the server's Bootstrap promotes the copy when it starts) or `Server: loads without a restart` (the remote route and a listing that declares none), and `Private games: load the client's copy at their next start`. A side the mod does not use, or the server while none is picked, is left out.

**Stale client state (HotDemo, fixed).** Live, HotDemo's Client cell read `Installed 0.1.0 · Managed elsewhere` although this client had no copy (Bootstrap had removed it at start). The evidence was the local server's promoted copy: the Client half read the listing-wide detection, which scans both roots, and a server-root `mod` ledger row with its file in the server's `progenitor-reload/` makes the listing "loaded". With no client ledger row the half then took the managed-elsewhere branch. The identity cache and the staging leftovers did not feed it. Each side now reads only its own evidence: `InstalledStateQuery.ForRoot` keeps one root's files and ledger rows (and the inventory, i.e. what this process loaded) and the Client half reads `InstallModsPane.DetectClient`. A fixture of the live rig fails without the fix.

**Abandoned downloads.** Every package download lands in `BepInEx/progenitor-staging/index/tmp/` as `<name>.zip.<guid>.part`; a finished one is only remembered in that catalog's memory, so a restart or a Core swap orphaned it (live: about 64 MB, 43 copies of HotDemo). When the Mods menu builds its catalog (once per Core generation), a background sweep deletes `.part` files there older than two hours that the catalog does not hold (`StagingTempSweep`), and logs `Install: staging sweep removed N abandoned download(s), X MB, from <folder>; kept K: <names>`. A running download (recent writes), a held zip, other files and subfolders stay.

**Readback.** Cells add `column` (`client` / `server`), `grayed` and `glyph`; a scroll text cell adds `noScroll`. The server cell's readback `buttons` can name `admin-only` and `authenticate`. The reader dialog's kind is `readme`, its block row `dialog-reader` / cell `reader`.

### Round 2 acceptance procedure (not run)

Authorized test session only, test client at the title screen, Mods workspace on **Install mods**; read with `mods_ui widget_read` / `rects`, screenshot each step.

1. Header: no Side column; the Client header blue with a blue top rule, the Server header red with a red rule; at 3840×1080 (1624 px row) the side columns 440 px and Mod 404 px.
2. Rows: client cells on a blue band, server cells on a red band; the side buttons are the game's popup sprite tinted blue / red (hover and press still swap sprites); installed words green with `✓` (or words alone — read the `Ui: glyph` log line), updates amber with `↑`, pending pale blue.
3. A client-only mod's Server cell and a server-only mod's Client cell grayed, reading `No server side` and `No client side` respectively; picker on None: every Server cell grayed, `No server selected`.
4. As a non-admin on a remote server: Server cells readable, not grayed, one dim `Admin only` per actionable cell; on the admin list without adminauth: `Authenticate as admin`, which runs adminauth and reads `Authenticating…`.
5. Drawer: the clicked row does not move on expand, collapse or switching (open a drawer, then open one further down: the second row stays put); no scroll bar; the wheel over it scrolls the list; height at most 280 px and the next row visible; facts two per line; `Read full description` opens the reader (title, one scroll bar, headings, Close; Escape closes).
6. Drawer note: per side, e.g. `Client: loads without a restart · Server: loads at the next server restart`.
7. HotDemo (removed from the client, on the test server): Client `Not installed`, Server `Installed 0.1.0` (Client `! Missing: the server has it` once any session on this client has inspected HotDemo 0.1.0; see INSTALLFAIL1).
8. After the Mods menu first builds its catalog: `Install: staging sweep removed …` in the client log and `progenitor-staging/index/tmp/` holding only recent parts.

## Confirmed installs: ledger, failures and downloads (INSTALLFAIL1)

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release), game-free tested, and live-verified for the adopt path** (2026-10-03, Steam client in game on the isolated test server, Core 2026.10.28.6 and .7). Live: with the earlier unrecorded `progenitor-pending/index/progenitor.hotdemo.dll` on disk, Client → Install → Yes logged `Install: planned Progenitor-HotDemo for client: RestartClientRequired` and `Install: wrote Progenitor-HotDemo 0.1.0 for client; loads at the next restart …`, the client `progenitor-install.json` gained the `package:Progenitor-HotDemo@0.1.0` / `progenitor.hotdemo` row, no new `.part` was written, the Client cell read `Pending game restart` with `Cancel install`, and it still did after a further Core hot swap. **Not live-verified:** a fresh download-and-write install, a failed install's `Install failed` surface, Cancel install removing the row, and the verified-side cache restoring `! Missing` after a swap. Tests: `InstallSilentFailureTests` (the real `Progenitor-HotDemo-0.1.0.zip` as a fixture, served from a local index like `_testserver/dummy`), `ServerInstallTests`.

**What happened live (2026-10-03, Steam client).** The Client cell's Install on HotDemo 0.1.0 was confirmed and the row kept `Missing: the server has it` with Install; nothing was logged after the dialog closed, while the bar later read `1 mod installed — restart the game to apply`. The install had in fact succeeded: `progenitor-pending/index/progenitor.hotdemo.dll` (the right bytes) was on disk from the first Yes, and each later Yes took the idempotent "already pending" branch. The write was never recorded: `InstallStaging.AppendServerLedger` returned early for a `progenitor.json` package on the `Client` target, so the client ledger stayed empty. The Client cell reads its half from the ledger, so it never saw the copy; the bar counts pending files found by the scan, so it did. An unledgered copy also cannot be cancelled or uninstalled from the menu.

**Now.**

- A restart install on the process's own root (and any `Client`-target write) is ledgered as `pending` on that root, with the listing key as `source` and the plugin id in `pluginId`, and the ledger is created when the root has none. This matches the hot and ordinary routes. Pressing Install again over an unledgered pending copy with the same bytes adopts it.
- Every outcome after the confirmation is logged by `InstallLog`: `Install: planned <key> for <side>: <decision>`, `Install: cannot install <key> …`, `Install: download of <key> failed: …`, `Install: <key> failed inspection: …`, `Install: wrote <package> <version> for <side>; loads at the next restart from <path>` (or `loaded now`), `Install: <key> was not installed: <reason>`, and a warning when a written file could not be ledgered. An exception inside the set write becomes a named failure instead of reaching the pane's catch.
- A confirmed install that writes nothing leaves its side's cell reading **Install failed** (warning tone) with Install to retry; the drawer shows the reason as its first line (`ActionError` in the readback).
- `PendingCount` also counts a client copy waiting for the game restart when the local server already loads its own copy, so the bar and the Client cell agree.
- Downloads: the `.part` zips a written set used are deleted when the write ends, either way; a second download of the same listing replaces the first; ending a flow without writing (No, Close, another listing) deletes its download. Files an ended process left are the staging sweep's job (above).
- The facts an inspection proved about a version's `progenitor.json` (its manifest and `side`) are kept in `BepInEx/progenitor-cache/verified/<namespace>/<name>/<version>.json`. A new catalog (a Core swap or a restart) re-applies them to listings it has not inspected, so a manifest-verified `side: both` row keeps its `! Missing` half instead of falling back to the listing's guess. Live, generation 7 showed `! Missing: the server has it` and generation 8 showed plain `Not installed`. The record only describes the row: an install still downloads, inspects and plans before it writes.

**Live check (not run).** On the Steam client with the dev build deployed, Install tab, HotDemo 0.1.0 row, server picker on the test server:

1. If `progenitor-pending/index/progenitor.hotdemo.dll` is still on disk from the failed sessions, press the Client cell's Install and confirm. The cell must read `Pending game restart`; the client `BepInEx/progenitor-install.json` must gain one `pending` row for that file (`source` `package:Progenitor-HotDemo@0.1.0`, `pluginId` `progenitor.hotdemo`); the log must show `Install: planned …` and `Install: wrote Progenitor-HotDemo 0.1.0 for client; loads at the next restart …`; and `progenitor-staging/index/tmp/` must hold no new HotDemo `.part`.
2. Cancel installing on the Client cell: the pending file and its ledger row go, and the cell returns to `! Missing: the server has it`.
3. Hot swap Core, then reopen the tab: HotDemo's Client cell still reads `! Missing: the server has it` (from `progenitor-cache/verified`), with no download in the log.

## HotDemo: hot install and uninstall, trusted hotLoadable (HOTDEMO1)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client in game on the isolated test server, Core 2026.10.28.7 / generation 12, branch fix/hotdemo-hot-path 32f825dc):** Server → Uninstall logged `Install: local server copy of Progenitor-HotDemo is unloaded now (trusted hotLoadable, reload folder watched).` and the server logged `Reload: unloaded Progenitor Hot Demo: clean` with no `.uninstall` marker left; Client → Install planned `ApplyNow`, wrote into `progenitor-reload` and logged `Reload: loaded Progenitor Hot Demo 0.1.0`, the cell reading `Installed 0.1.0`; Server → Install planned `ApplyNow` for the local server and the server logged `Reload: loaded Progenitor Hot Demo 0.1.0`; Client → Uninstall removed the file and logged `Reload: unloaded Progenitor Hot Demo: clean`; Client → Install again loaded it. No step needed a restart. **Not live-verified:** the stopped-server fallback to `progenitor-pending`, the refusal cases (hash mismatch, `hotLoadable: false`, unsafe images), and remote (non-local) servers.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Tests: `InstallSilentFailureTests` (section 4), `AdmissionTests`, `InstallHotLoadTests`.

**Why HotDemo went to the next restart (live 2026-10-03, Core 2026.10.28.7).** HotDemo declares `side: both, hotLoadable: true`. Its only qualification record was reviewed against `coreContract` `core/0.1.0+admission/1`. `coreContract` is Core's build version, so that record has expired with every Core build since. Admission returned `QualificationMismatch`, and the planner took the restart route.

**Policy (decided 2026-10-03): trust the hotLoadable flag.** A package that declares `hotLoadable: true` installs hot without a record when it passes inspection (declared `sha256` = the installable DLL) and its image is one clean `ProgenitorPlugin`. Clean means a resolved base chain and references, and no UnsafeHarmony, HarmonyLib, ClassInjector, native-import or reflection-escape finding. The admission reason is `TrustedHotLoadable`, and a missing or expired record is listed after it. Everything else keeps the restart route, with the reason at the end of the `Install: planned …` log line. The rules are in [mod-manifest.md](mod-manifest.md#qualification-records-authoring-the-only-route-to-apply-now-job-hot2).

**Client.**

- *Install:* the plan is `ApplyNow`. The image goes into `BepInEx/progenitor-reload/` and is ledgered as `mod`. The log reads `Install: wrote … for client; loaded now from …`, and the Client cell reads Installed with `Client: loads without a restart` in the drawer.
- *Uninstall of a loaded copy:* it unloads now when its bytes are the verified hotLoadable ones (`HotLoadTrust.CanUnloadHot`, which also covers a copy promoted at an earlier start).
- *Uninstall of a pending copy that never loaded:* it is deleted at once. The Client cell asks the client's own state, not the listing-wide one, so the local server's copy no longer makes it read `Removal pending game restart`.

**Local server (local-folder route).**

- *What the client checks:* the client cannot ask the server's loader anything, so it reads what it can see (`HotLoadTrust.ProbeLocalServer`): a `VRisingServer.exe` running from that folder (matched by full path, never signaled), Progenitor Core in its `progenitor-core/`, and no `config/progenitor-allow.txt`.
- *With all three:* the server half is written into the server's `progenitor-reload/` (ledgered `mod`, no `hot` flag), and that server's reloader loads it within a second (`Install: wrote … into the local server's reload folder`). Its Uninstall deletes the file, so the reloader unloads it, and any stale `.uninstall` marker beside it goes too. The drawer reads `Server: loads without a restart`.
- *Without them:* the pending file and the marker, as before, and the log says which check failed.
- *Still a restart:* a copy of the plugin already in the server's reload folder makes an install a replacement, which stays on the restart route.

**Live check (not run).** Start from the current state: Client `Not installed`, Server `Removal pending server restart` with Cancel uninstall. The test server is running in `_testserver\server`.

1. Deploy this build and open the Install tab with the picker on the local test server folder.
2. Server cell → **Uninstall** is not offered while the marker stands, so press **Cancel uninstall** first, then Server → Uninstall → Yes. Expected: `Install: local server copy of Progenitor-HotDemo is unloaded now (…)`, `_testserver\server\BepInEx\progenitor-reload\progenitor.hotdemo.dll` gone, no `.uninstall` beside it, a `Reload:` unload line in the **server** log within a second, and the Server cell `Not installed`.
3. Client → Install → Yes. Expected: `Install: planned Progenitor-HotDemo for client: ApplyNow …`, `Install: wrote Progenitor-HotDemo 0.1.0 for client; loaded now from …\progenitor-reload\progenitor.hotdemo.dll`, a client `Reload:` load line, and the Client cell `Installed 0.1.0` with no restart note.
4. Install on both (or Server → Install) → Yes. Expected: `wrote … into the local server's reload folder`, a server `Reload:` load line, and the Server cell Installed.
5. Client → Uninstall → Yes: the client file goes and the reloader unloads it within a second. Repeat for the server: the file goes and is unloaded on the server, with no restart wording on either cell.
6. Stop the test server, then Server → Install: the plan line names `the local server is not running` and the half goes to `progenitor-pending` (restart route).

## Installs from the join screens (JOINFLOW2)

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested (`JoinCatalogInstallTests`, `JoinFlowStage2Tests`); not live-verified.**

The [join screens](join-flow.md#4-installing-from-thunderstore-stage-2) install the Thunderstore packages a server names with this tab's machinery, not a second downloader: per batch an `InstallCatalogService` built like this tab's (the client root only, the same admission pipeline, `registerForBridge: false` so `install_local` keeps reading this tab's catalog), loaded from the same Thunderstore index (fetched, else this tab's cached `BepInEx/progenitor-cache/index.json`) with `[Install] LocalIndexPath` merged in front. Each row then runs what a confirmed Install here runs: `DownloadAndInspectAsync` (the `MaxDownloadMB` cap, the mod-manager refusal), `PlanWithDependenciesAsync` for the client, and `ExecuteWithDependencies` on the main thread, so the trusted hotLoadable admission (`TrustedHotLoadable`) decides between `progenitor-reload` (loads now) and `progenitor-pending` (next start), the write is ledgered in `progenitor-install.json`, and `InstallLog` writes the same `Install: planned …` / `Install: wrote …` lines.

What the join adds:

- **The exact version.** A server names `Owner-Name` and the version its handshake requires. The package's version list is read (`ThunderstoreVersions`; up to 1,024 versions, active ones only) and that version's entry replaces the newest in the batch's catalog (`InstallCatalogService.UseVersionEntry`). A version that is gone is refused, never replaced by a newer one.
- **Refusals the join needs:** `[Mods] UseThunderstore` off; `[Mods] ShowNsfwContent` off for an author-flagged package; a fetched listing whose download is not `https` on `thunderstore.io` or its hosts (`NotThunderstore`; the local index is allowed); a server-only package (`SideMismatch`); a package whose plugin is not the mod the server named; an Update or "Use version" over a copy MANAGED1 reads as **Managed elsewhere**; a downloaded zip whose size is not the listing's.
- **Progress.** `ProgressCatalogTransport` counts the bytes of the responses read under the row's `JoinDownloadProgress.Current` (an `AsyncLocal`, so two rows downloading at once each see their own): `Downloading 62%` on the row. A `file://` download from the local index reports no percentage.
- **At most two at a time;** plans and writes one at a time.

This tab's own rows and catalog are untouched by it; the files and ledger rows a join install writes are the ones this tab reads, so the Install tab shows them as its own installs afterwards.

## Kept versions (JOINFLOW4)

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested (`ModVersionStoreTests`); not live-verified.**

When the player is ahead of a server, the join flow keeps their newer copy of a mod in `BepInEx/progenitor-versions/` instead of replacing it ([join-flow.md](join-flow.md#9-keeping-both-versions-stage-4)). The workspace shows what is kept:

- **The mod page** (between the head and the Settings section, with the page's other notes): `Other versions kept: 1.9.0 (yours) · 1.8.2 (server's)` — every kept version, newest first, `(yours)` on the player's own copies and preferred version, `(server's)` on a copy a server's install placed — and `· 1.8.2 loads at the next start` while a swap is queued. Under it **Restore 1.9.0** when the player's own version is kept and not active: it swaps that copy back in now (or queues it for the next start when the reloader cannot unload the active one) and makes it the preferred version; the page reopens with the answer as its note. A refusal (the kept file changed on disk, the allowlist, two copies, managed elsewhere, a join in progress) is the note instead.
- **Settings** ends with a **Kept versions** group (`Ui/KeptVersions.cs`): one line per mod (`<id>: Other versions kept: …`) and the same **Restore** button. (The **Server mods** group with its Remove, which it used to follow, was removed 2026-10-05 with server-sent mod files; Core start deletes kept copies a server sent.)

The **install ledger** and the legacy **server-mods record** list the active copy only: parking a copy takes its rows with it, and a Restore puts them back under the file it lands as. So the Install tab, the Mods sidebar and Remove see exactly what is loaded. The Install tab's drawer does not list kept versions.

A copy loaded from `BepInEx/plugins` (another tool's install, **Managed elsewhere**) is never kept or swapped.

## Ordinary plugins in the Thunderstore layout (INSTPLUG1)

**Live (2026-10-03, Steam client in game on the isolated test server, Core generation 14, branch fix/install-bepinex-plugins-layout dc57e511):** Install on a third-party plain BepInEx plugin package (Client) logged `Install: planned <package> for client: RestartClientRequired (1 package(s)): ordinary BepInEx package …` and `Install: wrote <package> <version> for client; loads at the next restart from …\progenitor-pending\index\<plugin>.dll`; the client ledger gained `BepInEx/progenitor-pending/index/<plugin>.dll` with `source` `package:<package>@<version>`; `BepInEx\plugins\` gained nothing; the Client cell read `Pending game restart` with `Cancel install`. **Not live-verified:** the move into `progenitor-reload` at the next game start, and the named-path refusals.

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release) and game-free tested; not live-verified.** Tests: `InstallBepInExPluginsLayoutTests`, which uses a generated plain BepInEx plugin package (`QATeam-QStacks-1.0.1.zip`, built by `tools/make-dummy-package.ps1 -Kind Plain`) as a fixture, and `InstallOrdinaryPackageTests`.

**Live (2026-10-03).** Install on a third-party plain BepInEx plugin package read `Cannot install — the package layout targets BepInEx/plugins/, patchers/ or core/; this menu will not write there`. The plugin was in the standard place, `BepInEx/plugins/<name>/<name>.dll`, which the ordinary restart route already accepts. The route refused the package for its other files: `SOURCE_REVIEW.md` at the root (not one of the fixed root names: README, manifest, icon, changelog, license, notice) and the plugin's own source under `Source/`. The planner then showed the generic layout sentence instead of that refusal.

**Now.**

- **Left out, never placed:** files that are paperwork rather than runtime content. These are any root file with a documentation, image or source extension (`.md .txt .pdf .png .jpg .jpeg .gif .webp .cs .csproj .sln .props .targets .yml .yaml .pdb`, editor and git files), the same extensions in a folder outside `BepInEx/` (`Source/`, `docs/`), and `.pdb` or `.md` beside the plugin under `BepInEx/plugins/`.
- **Still refused, by name:**
  - a data file beside the plugin (`BepInEx/plugins/QStack/stacks.json`), since the plugin may read it;
  - anything else under `BepInEx/`;
  - anything under `patchers/`, `core/` or `config/` (with or without `BepInEx/`).
- **Wording:** a refused ordinary package's plan reason is the route's own sentence, naming the entry (`this package writes BepInEx/config/QStack.cfg (under patchers/, core/ or config/) …`). The generic layout sentence names the first offending entry too.
- **Where QStacks goes:** it installs like a root-level DLL. `QStack.dll` goes to `BepInEx/progenitor-pending/index/` (restart route: it is not a Progenitor plugin) and gets a `pending` ledger row with `source` `package:QATeam-QStacks@1.0.1` and `pluginId` = its `[BepInPlugin]` GUID. The menu never writes to `BepInEx/plugins/`.

**Live check (not run).** Install mods → a plain BepInEx plugin package (the generated `QATeam-QStacks` fixture served from a local index, or any such package) → Client Install → Yes.

- The client log should read `Install: planned QATeam-QStacks for client: RestartClientRequired (1 package(s)): ordinary BepInEx package …` and then `Install: wrote QATeam-QStacks 1.0.1 for client; loads at the next restart from …\progenitor-pending\index\QStack.dll`.
- The Client cell should read `Pending game restart`.
- The client `progenitor-install.json` should have the pending row described above.
- `BepInEx\plugins\` should be unchanged.
- After a game restart, Bootstrap promotes it into `progenitor-reload/` and the cell reads Installed.

## Install rows per side (INSTALLSIDES1)

Status: **source-checked, built (Debug and Release), game-free tested, and partly live-verified** (2026-10-03, Steam client at 3840×1080, in game on the isolated test server and at the title, Core 2026.10.26.3 to .6). Seen live: per-side Client and Server cells with vanilla chevrons and buttons, `Not needed` on the unused half, the Server header naming the picked server, the picker as a vanilla popup listing `Connected: Progenitor Forms Test` (the same server's local folder merged into it), `Progenitor Headless Test (this PC)`, `Private games (this PC)` and `None · this client only`, `Same as client` under Private games, the drawer opening only from its chevron with the row moved to the top, rail, seam and Collapse, the next rows below the drawer body after the reservation fix, README headings kept across two Core hot swaps (README-STRUCT1, `<version>.md` written), and the NSFW switch present and off on Configuration → Sources. **Not live-verified:** the mouse wheel over the drawer body and the list while a drawer is open, the NSFW filter hiding a flagged listing, every side button's confirm box, the update states, and the mod page band. Where it disagrees with the older sections below ([Install list columns](#install-list-columns), the LIST2 drawer bullet, the **Default install target** row, the Status column tables in [Server installs](#server-installs-one-server-target) and [Per-side status](#per-side-status-both-sided-mods)), this section is current. Tests: `SideCellsTests`, `ServerPickerTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests`, `InstallNsfwFilterTests`, plus the updated `InstallColumnsTests`, `InstallListViewTests` and `InstallDownloadFlowTests`.

"Option A" was approved on 2026-10-03: one row says, separately, what this client has and what the picked server has, with a button per side.

**Columns.** `[expand] Mod | Side | Client | Server | Released | Downloads` (`InstallColumns`). Version and Installed columns are gone (the drawer carries both facts). At the live 1236 px row: expand 28, icon 36, Mod fills (216 px; floor 150), Side 72, Client 300, Server 300, Released 98, Downloads 118. Narrow panes drop Released, then Downloads; the active sort column never drops. Sort: Mod (A to Z), Client (installed date, the old Installed sort), Released, Downloads.

**Mod cell.** Name over `by <owner>`; the update note leads the second line as before. Clicking the row does nothing: only the expand arrow (`<key>/expand`) opens and closes the drawer.

**Side cells.** One policy, `SideCells.Decide` ([`src/Progenitor.Core/Install/SideCells.cs`](../src/Progenitor.Core/Install/SideCells.cs)), gives each side's words, tone and up to two buttons (`client-a`/`client-b`, `server-a`/`server-b`), and the shortcut (`both`).

| Situation | Client cell | Server cell |
|---|---|---|
| Side does not use this half (client-only mod on a server, server-only mod on the client while a server is picked) | `Not needed` (muted) | `Not needed` (muted) |
| Absent | `Not installed`, **Install** | `Not installed`, **Install** when the route can install; else the route's note with no button: `Ask a server admin` (not admin / not allowed), `Server can't reach Thunderstore`, `Remote installs off`, `No Progenitor on server`, `Checking server…`, `No server` |
| Installed, current | `Installed 1.2.0` (ok), **Uninstall** when a ledger owns the copy | same, **Uninstall** when the route can manage |
| Installed, behind the listing | `1.1.0 · update 1.2.0` (warn), **Update**, **Uninstall** | same, route-gated |
| Pending | `Pending game restart` / `Pending server restart`, **Cancel install** | same, route-gated |
| Removal pending | `Removal pending …`, **Cancel uninstall** | same, route-gated |
| Busy (download, remote step) | step words, **Cancel** variant only when the step can be cancelled | same |
| Managed elsewhere | `Installed 1.2.0 · Managed …` (muted), no button | — |
| Manifest-verified both-sided mod, other side missing | `Missing: the server has it` (warn), **Install** | `Missing: the client has it` (warn) |
| Manifest-verified both-sided mod, this side older than the other | `older than server` (warn), **Update** | `older than client` |
| Server half unknown (handshake only, no rows) | — | `Unknown` (muted) |
| Picked "None" | — | `No server` (muted) |
| Picked "Private games", shared plugins folder (or a Progenitor-managed copy) | — | `Same as client` (muted) |
| Picked "Private games", separate plugins folder, client mod | — | `Not loaded there` (warn) |
| Not installable (tool, refused) | `Cannot install` / the refusal | — |

Only a manifest-verified `side: both` declaration produces `Missing` / `older` words; a listing Thunderstore merely tags Client + Server never warns. **Install on both** / **Update both** (a box-less gold link under the two cells) appears only when both sides would show Install, or both Update. Every button routes to the existing confirmations and executors (`INSTALLUX2`), scoped to its side: a Client **Uninstall** removes only the client copy, a Server one only the server copy, and the box names that side only. An **Update** button's box always reads as an update.

**Server picker.** The toolbar carries `Server: <pick> ▾` (`toolbar/serverPick`), drawn with the game's dropdown bar. It opens a popup choice (`Act on which server?`) listing, in order (`ServerPicker.Build`):

1. `Connected: <name>` — the server this client is connected to (the default whenever connected). Its name is the one the install confirmations use: when a dedicated-server folder on this PC is that server (the route selector proved it is the configured folder, or exactly one found folder's `ServerHostSettings.json` port is the joined port and the joined address is this machine), that folder's name (`-serverName`, else the settings file's `Name`), and the folder is **not listed again**; otherwise the name in the server's signed catalog, unless it is the catalog default `V Rising`; otherwise the address. The address stays secondary: beside the name in the list when the caption fits one popup button (44 characters), and always in the dialog's line `Connected to <name> at <address>[, a server on this PC].` A folder picked earlier that turns out to be the connected server resolves to this entry. The install confirmations for the remote route name the server the same way.
2. `<name> (this PC)` — each local dedicated server found by the existing discovery (`SteamLibraryScan.SuggestDetailed`, cached 30 s) plus the configured folder; picking one validates it and writes `[Install] DedicatedServerPath`, refusing with a message when the folder is not a server.
3. `Private games (this PC)` — the hosted private-game server. With the launcher's **Shared** plugins mode its cell reads `Same as client`; with **ProgenitorOnly** (separate plugins folder) client mods read `Not loaded there`.
4. `None · this client only` — Server cells read `No server`.

Default when nothing was picked: connected server, else the configured local folder, else private games. The pick lasts for the session. The Server column header names the pick (`Server · <name>`). The old `[Install] DefaultTarget` key still loads (old files keep working) but no longer routes rows and is no longer shown on Configuration.

**Drawer reservation (live fix, 2026-10-03).** The first live review (Core 2026.10.26.3, 3840×1080) showed the next listing rows drawn under the drawer: the body (about 460 px at the 708 px viewport) was drawn at its height, but the panel reserved at most `PanelLayout.MaxRowHeight` (400 px) for any row. The body's own `RectMask2D` was clipping its text at the body's bottom; the rows below had been placed inside it. A fixed cell row now reserves its own height, up to `PanelLayout.MaxFixedRowHeight` (2000 px); measured rows keep the 400 px cap. Game-free tested (`PanelLayoutTests`); not re-verified live.

**Drawer.** Opened only by the expand arrow (the arrow turns down), closed by it or by **Collapse** at the drawer's top left; one drawer at a time. (INSTALLSIDES2: opening no longer scrolls; the clicked row stays where it is.) The drawer joins its row: the row's gold accent rail continues down the drawer, the drawer shares the row's selected fill through the gap, its content is indented to the name column (80 px), and a thin gold seam separates the row from the drawer. Head row: **Collapse**, the note, **Read full description**, **Configure in Mods**, **Restart now** when it applies. Body: a fixed-height scrolling text block (`CellKind.ScrollText`) with the game's scroll bar, sized so row + drawer is about 80% of the list viewport while the next row stays at least 40 px visible (`InstallDrawerLayout.BodyHeight`; never taller than its text, never under 96 px). The mouse wheel over the block scrolls it first and passes through to the list at its ends. Body lines: route and side notes, restart help, one-liner, stale-update warning, then facts — Latest version, Released, Downloads, Side, Installed here (`Not on this PC`), Requires, NSFW content (`Flagged by the author`, only when flagged), Package, Plugin, Update tag — then the README (capped until **Read full description**).

**README structure across restarts and hot swaps (README-STRUCT1, 2026-10-03).** The drawer draws README headings and code from the markdown. The fetched documents live in the Core generation's memory, and the disk cache (`progenitor-cache/readme/<owner>/<package>/<version>.txt`) held only the plain search text, so in a later process or after a Core hot swap every heading came back as a paragraph (seen live: bold "Features" on generation 1, plain on generation 2). This predates INSTALLSIDES1 and is on `main` too. The markdown is now cached beside the text (`<version>.md`, same release-stamp line) and the drawer renders from it with no request.
