**Status: built and game-free tested (`ServerModUninstallTests`, the SRVUNINST1 cases in `RemoteInstallServiceTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests`, `FrameworkAllowlistTests`), not live-verified.** Live reports, 2026-10-07, Steam client joined to the isolated test server as an admin: (1) a third-party command library, installed only on the server, showed **Uninstall** on its Mods page; the box promised removal from "your local server", and **Yes** ran only the client's ledger seam, which refused with `Progenitor did not install this mod, so it cannot remove it.` (2) HotDemo read `✓ Installed` with **Uninstall** in Simple; its Uninstall went to the server, which had only a stale ledger row for an old file name, and the refusal (`can't read Thunderstore's index … uninstall again with force`) appeared only in the log.

- **The Mods page decides each side before it asks** (`ModUninstallPlan`). The client copy: the FWALLOW1 reload-folder removal, or the ledger seam when it owns the plugin (`IModUninstallSeam.Owns`). The server copy, by the Install tab's route: on the remote route, the connected server's ledger row for the listing, else its `plugin:<guid>` row, sent through the same admin request the Install tab sends ([Admin remote installs](mod-package-capabilities.md#admin-remote-installs)); on the local-folder route, the seam's local-server half. A server-only row (`On this server`) offers Uninstall too.
- **Nothing removable, no confirmation.** When no side can be removed from here, **Uninstall** is drawn dim and takes no click, and the muted note under the header says why: not an admin there (the route's own sentence), `<server> did not say which files are this mod's`, `Managed elsewhere on <server>`, `Already removed from <server> at its next restart`, `Join the server as an admin to remove its copy.`, or the client's `Progenitor did not install this mod, so it cannot remove it.`
- **The box says what will happen.** Client copy: today's sentences. Server copy, from the server's row: `Removed from <server> now; its reloader unloads it, nothing to restart.` (a reload-folder or hot copy), `Removed from <server> at its next restart (or now, if it was hot loaded there).` (a loaded copy: a ledgered one, which the server unloads now when its bytes are a verified hotLoadable plugin, or an ordinary plugin the server moves to `BepInEx/progenitor-removed` at its next start; the row cannot tell the two apart, so the sentence fits both), `The copy waiting on <server> is deleted now; …` (a pending copy). A server-only listing adds `Players without it are not affected.` The local folder's copy is named by its server (`Also removed from <name> at its next restart.`), never "your local server". A side that stays is named as staying.
- **Yes does what the box said.** The plan is kept when the box opens (and when the open page is redrawn); if Yes finds a different plan, nothing is sent and the note reads `Changed since the box opened, so nothing was done. Check the page and try again.`
- **The answer is the page's note.** The note follows the request (`Sent to <server>…`), then the server's answer (`Removed from <server>.`, `Removed from <server> at its next restart.`, or `<server> did not remove it: <reason>.`), and the open page is rebuilt with the server's new rows. A refusal the server allows to override ends `Uninstall again to remove it without that check.`; the next Uninstall then says the dependency check is skipped and sends `force`.
- **Install tab.** A refused remote uninstall stays on the Server cell (`Installed 0.1.0 · Uninstall refused`) and in the drawer (`<server> did not remove it: <reason>.`); when the server allows the override the cell offers **Uninstall anyway**, whose box says the dependency check is skipped. Simple shows the same row with **Uninstall anyway**. A client copy that is a reload-folder DLL no ledger lists reads `Installed 0.1.0 · in progenitor-reload` with **Uninstall** (the Mods page's FWALLOW1 removal and box), never `Managed elsewhere`; a whole-row Uninstall takes it too, and its box then carries a **This client** fact with that copy's own sentence (the reload file and every plugin it holds, or the preloader's move); when nothing here can remove it the fact reads `Stays: nothing here can remove this copy.` and Yes leaves it. Yes acts on the copy the box showed: if it changed meanwhile, nothing is done and the drawer says `Changed since the box opened, so nothing was done. Check the page and try again.`
- **Install records without files.** A ledger `mod` row whose file is gone (the HotDemo case: an old DLL name) is not an install on either side (`ServerInstall.IsOrphanRow`, read by the client's scan and the server's rows). Such a record stays when it is the package's only one (the reconcile pass never un-ledgers a missing file on its own: another tool may have moved it) and is invisible in both tabs; a remote uninstall request (`remote_install uninstall <package>` on the bridge) drops it without the index check and touches no file, and the next remote install of the package clears it first. REQRULE4 (live 2026-10-08: the test server's ledger still listed HotDemo's pre-rename DLL name beside the current row): the reconcile drops an orphan row when a row of the same package, or of the same package name under another owner (F38, live 2026-10-08: the stale row was `OldOwner-HotDemo`, the current one `Progenitor-HotDemo`, so the same-key rule never fired), written at the same time or later names a file that is on disk (`ServerInstall.SupersededOrphans`, `SamePackageName`). On the local route the client runs it on the server folder's ledger when the Install catalog opens and on every changed ledger; the server runs it on its own ledger before each remote status listing (`RemoteInstallService.ListRows`). Log: `Install: dropped the stale install record <path> (<source>): ...`. Chosen over a Clean up install records action because the superseded row carries no information the newer row lacks and needs no confirmation; an orphan with no live sibling is still kept. Only ledger rows are dropped, never files. Game-free tested (`InstallConfirmTests`); not live-verified.
- **"Unloads now" means now** (live 2026-10-07: a join-installed HotDemo, hot loaded, read `Unloads now; nothing to restart.` and was marked for the next start). The box is worded from the seam's own decision (`IModUninstallSeam.UnloadsNow`), and the seam unloads a copy now when the reloader holds it and either the ledger's `hot` flag is set or every ledgered copy in this client's `progenitor-reload` is the trusted hotLoadable image an inspection verified (HOTDEMO1, the Install tab's and the server's rule; a join-window install sets no flag, and a new start clears it). A copy Bootstrap promoted at start that is not such an image is still marked. The note then says what happened: `Removed`, or `Removes at the next restart; it stays loaded until then.` The removal log names the server only when a local server folder is configured. Built and game-free tested, not live-verified.
- **The listing the install record names** (F36, live 2026-10-08: `progenitor.film` was installed from the local package Progenitor-FilmRestart while Progenitor-FilmExample named the same plugin id; Uninstall said `Removes at the next restart; it stays loaded until then.` and nothing was marked or logged). The seam now resolves a plugin through the package its ledger rows name before the index's plugin-id match; when the ledger names two packages for one plugin, no listing is guessed and the ledger rows themselves are acted on. A seam answer that changed nothing never reads as success: the note is `Nothing was changed: no installed copy of this mod matched its install record.` Every confirmed Uninstall, Reactivate and Cancel install logs `Ui: Mods page <action> of <plugin>: <note>`. Built and game-free tested (`InstallConfirmTests`), not live-verified.
- **The row after a reload-folder removal** (live 2026-10-07: HotDemo still read `Installed 0.1.0 · Managed elsewhere` 8 s after its file was deleted and unloaded). The Install rows read the scan the tab was built with, whose inventory still listed the loaded plugin. A removal through FWALLOW1 (from either tab) now hands the Install rows a fresh scan with the inventory read again, at once and again after the reloader's next scan (`InstallModsPane.NoteClientCopyRemoved`). Built and game-free tested, not live-verified.
- **Versions in the page's words.** A server-only row's inventory version is display text (`Server 0.1.0`, `Local 1.0; server 1.1`); the page, its status bar and its confirmation now use the bare version (`ModMenuInventory.BareVersion`): `Uninstall progenitor.reloadexample 0.1.0?`, `v0.1.0`. The side stays in the sides chip.
- **Readback.** `page-framework` adds `actionEnabled` and `actionReason`; `page-action` adds `enabled` and `reason`; `page-uninstall` is the plan (`canAct`, `client`, `server`, `remotePackage`, `remoteState`, `remoteForce`, `serverName`, `why`, `body`). Side cells may carry the button name `uninstall-anyway`.

### Live check (not run)

On the isolated test server, joined as an admin on its `adminlist.txt`, with `[Install] TreatLocalServerAsRemote = true` when the client's `DedicatedServerPath` is that server's folder (otherwise the local-folder route decides). Use a disposable sample DLL copied into the server's `BepInEx\progenitor-reload` with no ledger row, and keep a copy to restore.

1. Client `remote_install status`: the route is `remote`; the server's rows include `plugin:<guid>` with state `hot`.
2. `mods_ui open` (`site` as the session needs), `select_tab mods`, `widget_click label=mod:<guid>`, `widget_read pane=content`: `page-uninstall.server` is `remote`, `remotePackage` `plugin:<guid>`, `canAct` true; `page-action.enabled` true.
3. `widget_click label=Uninstall`, then `mods_ui status`: the dialog body says `Removed from <server> now; its reloader unloads it, nothing to restart.` and does not say "local server". `dialog_choose yes`.
4. Client log: `Install: remote Uninstall plugin:<guid> sent to the connected server (request N).` then `… finished: Removed`. Server log: `Install: remote uninstall of plugin <guid>: ReloadNow.`, the audit line `op=uninstall package=plugin:<guid> … result=removed`, and the reloader's unload. The page note reads `Removed from <server>.`; the file is gone from the server's `progenitor-reload`.
5. As a non-admin (or with `[RemoteInstall] Enabled = false`): the same page's `page-action.enabled` is false, `reason` names the block, and clicking Uninstall opens no dialog.
6. Install tab, Advanced: a client-only reload-folder copy (a sample in the client's `progenitor-reload`) reads `Installed … · in progenitor-reload` with **Uninstall**.
7. Restore the sample DLL on the server, remove any client sample you added, close the workspace, disconnect, and leave the client at the title screen.

**Fresh state after every action (F2, F7, live 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested, not live-verified).** After Cancel uninstall, the page header kept `Client: Removal pending game restart` and the list banner kept `1 mod change applies after a restart`. After an immediate uninstall, the page showed `Enabled`, `Removal pending`, `Installed by another tool` and `Removed` at once. Turning a hot-loaded client mod off left `1 mod loads after a restart` showing, even after it was turned back on.

- Every Mods-page action and the list switch now read their state again (`ModsMenu.RefreshAfterPageAction`: the inventory, and the Install tab's installed-state scan that the side cells and the restart banner read).
- A mod whose last copy is gone after the action is no longer in the list, so the workspace returns to the list.
- A mod the switch holds off (a hot suspend) is not counted as waiting for a restart.
- The reloader's suspend line no longer says `for this server`, because the same suspend serves the switch and the per-server pause. This changes Bootstrap, so it applies after a restart.

**The local server folder's installs in the list (F3, live 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested, not live-verified).** At the title screen, the local server folder's command library and its dependent mod were listed among this client's installed mods. They come from that folder's install ledger, not from a previous server. An install only the local server folder's ledger names, with no copy on this client and no joined server claiming it, is now listed in the server group. While not connected, the group reads `On your local server`. A joined server's claim still folds into the mods as before (POLISH2). Live 2026-10-08: the readback listed those rows in the server group but the list drew no header or rows, because the list counted rows by origin rather than by the group they are listed in; it now counts by the listed group (`ListProvenance`; built and source-checked in `ModsMenuPolish2Tests`, not live-verified).

**Exactly the sides named, each by its own state (F4, F10, live 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested, not live-verified).** The Mods page used to hand one decision to the uninstall seam for every root. A client-only uninstall then also acted on the configured local server folder, without the box saying so, and logged `(client and the local server)`. A server copy just hot loaded into the local server folder was marked for its next start, because "now" was read from this client's reloader. Now Yes removes exactly the sides the box names (`IModUninstallSeam.UninstallSides`):

- **This client's copy** goes now when this process can unload it, else at the next start.
- **The local server folder's copy** goes now when that server's own reloader holds a trusted hot-unloadable image (`ServerUnloadsNow`, the Install tab's HOTDEMO1 rule), else at its next restart.

The box says which: `Removed from <server> now; its reloader unloads it, nothing to restart.` or `… at its next restart.` Each side logs its own line (`(client)`, `(local server)`).

**From the ledger alone, and never from a stale state (F26, F27, live 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested in `InstallConfirmTests`, not live-verified).**

- **A listing that left the index.** Its mod has no Install-tab row, and its Mods page could neither cancel nor uninstall it: Yes did nothing and logged nothing. The uninstall seam now acts on the mod's install-ledger rows alone, by their `package:` source and root (`InstallCatalogService.ActOnLedgerRows`): a pending row is cancelled alone (`ServerInstall.RemovePendingOnly`: that version's written-but-not-loaded files only; review M4: a loaded copy of the same package, at another version or promoted, is never touched), a loaded copy is marked for its next start, and Reactivate drops the mark. A removal deletes only ledgered paths inside the root's BepInEx folder (review L9). Each row logs `Install: <verb> <source> (from its ledger row) (<side>)`.
- **A local server copy that already loaded.** At the title it read `Pending restart · to load` after that server had promoted and loaded it at its restart. Before the page decides about a mod with a pending ledger row, the roots' recorded promotions are folded into the ledgers (`ModsMenu.ReconcileIfPending`, at most every 2 s), so the page reads what is on disk.
- **A Cancel install of a copy that already loaded** never deletes it. The answer reads `It already loaded at its restart, so there is no download to cancel; uninstall it instead.` and is logged.

**Before the Install catalog has loaded (F1, live 2026-10-07; built and game-free tested, not live-verified).** On a fresh Core the Mods page called a join-installed HotDemo "Progenitor did not install this mod", with Uninstall disabled and its pending removal hidden, until Install Mods had been opened once. Two changes fix this:

- The page serves the cached index and merges the configured local index before it decides about a ledgered mod (`ModsMenu.EnsureCatalogForLedger`).
- The uninstall seam also finds a plugin by the package its own install-ledger rows name (`CatalogUninstallSeam.LedgerKeysProbe`), not only by a catalog listing that names its plugin id.

A mod its ledger names is never called "not ours". If even its package is not in the catalog, the reason reads `Progenitor installed this mod, but its listing is not loaded yet; open Install Mods once, then uninstall it here.`

### Uninstalling a mod the server requires (UNINSTREQ1)

**Status: built and game-free tested (`ServerModUninstallTests`, `InstallSidesPaneTests`), not live-verified.** Standing rule (user, 2026-10-07): every uninstall confirmation that removes this client's copy of a mod the connected server requires says what happens next. This covers the Mods page, the Install tab's Client cell, and the whole-row box that Simple and Advanced share. "Requires" means the server's handshake asks for the mod as must-have, or its remembered client mod policy lists it as required, while this client is in its world. The box draws a `Required by` fact (warning tone). The value follows the server's own rule. The rule arrives on the handshake request's capability row and is updated by each notice the server sends; see [client-mod-policy.md](client-mod-policy.md). The wording, for a mod the client mod policy requires on a server that re-checks mods while you play:

- Disconnect: `<server>: it disconnects players who remove a mod it requires unless it's back within 60 seconds; your next join is refused until it's back.` (the server's Seconds to fix a mod as its rule message said them; 15 from a server that never sent one). The rule and the seconds follow a mid-session change (REQRULE2, [client-mod-policy.md](client-mod-policy.md#stage-3-as-built)).
- Warn: `<server>: you'll be warned in game and can keep playing; your next join is refused until it's back.`
- An older server that does not say: `<server>: it checks your mods while you play, so you'll be warned in game (or disconnected, if its admins chose that); your next join is refused until it's back.`

A must-have row of the server's join check is judged on every answer this client sends, so it never reads "nothing changes" (review 2026-10-07). Its clause is `it checks this mod whenever this client answers it again (after a Progenitor update, for one), and that answer would disconnect you`. It stands alone for a handshake-only requirement and is added after the rule's clause when the policy requires the mod too. Only a mod the policy alone requires, on a server that does not re-check mods while you play, reads `<server>: nothing checks it again while you stay, but your next join is refused until it's back.` A box that removes only the server's copy draws no fact. Readback: the Install prompt's `facts`. The Mods page dialog draws the fact under its body.

## Close and back

| Action | Contract |
|---|---|
| **Close Mods** / shortcut while open | `ModsMenu.Close()`: disposes the right pane, menu-hosted settings and the sidebar. An owned MODS tab click while open is a no-op (stay open, same workspace tab). When the shortcut opened the workspace over a FullscreenMenu it raised, the menu is then dismissed to gameplay ([Shortcut in a world](#shortcut-in-a-world)). |
| Native tab (same or other) | An owned `PointerClick` observer on each native outer tab calls `ModsMenu.Close()` if Mods is open. Native handlers stay intact, so the selected tab still opens. Same-tab click also closes: selecting the already-selected native tab does not change the hosted view. |
| HUD / menu instance change, or hosted-view identity change after a 30-frame settle | `ModsMenu.Close()`. |
| Client world ended, connection-ended, or handshake epoch change | `ModsMenu.Close()` through Handshake's `ServerContextCleared` notification: panes disposed, curtain dropped, input capture released, HUD observers released. Logs `Mods workspace: closed reason=world-ended` or `reason=server-context-cleared` once. NativeModsEntry re-evaluates the main-menu entry; a workspace opened from the shortcut in gameplay does not survive into the main menu. A Core-only refresh of a surviving world does not close. |
| **Escape** / Bootstrap `Back` | On a focused search box: clear, then leave the box, then close ([SEARCHCLEAR1](#clearing-a-search-searchclear1)); a clear or a leave never closes. The *close* half **hides** every shown Progenitor panel, then `ModsMenu.NotifyHiddenByEscape` logs `hide reason=escape`. On the in-game tab site it also dismisses FullscreenMenu (`HUDMenuManager.GoToMenu(None)`), so Escape returns to the game the way it does from Spellbook / Social. Main-menu and a search-clear do not. It does **not** call `ModsMenu.Close()` or `CloseOrBack` — Close and Back stay the explicit dispose / back-to-mod paths. Hidden handles linger until the next `Take()` (the next Open or Close). `IsOpen` then reads false, so the curtain and workspace backdrop drop. (A press while the workspace was suspended for the consent prompt was a no-op; that prompt was removed 2026-10-05.) With `WhileShown` capture registered, that press does not open the vanilla system menu. |
| Custom page **Back to …** | Reopens that mod’s overview. |
| Settings **Back to mod** | Closes the settings pane and reopens that mod’s overview. |
| Failed-page **Back to Mods** | Reopens the directory overview. |

**Curtain.** Not an overlay panel. `progenitor-ui-core-mods-background` sits at sorting order 4997 (the blocker slot of the Mods canvas band), copies the native `CanvasScaler`, and stretches an `Image` with `raycastTarget = true`. There is no click handler: the curtain eats clicks (including at alpha 0) and does not close Mods. It is destroyed on the same frame as `ModsMenu.Close()` / release / lifecycle (not the next Tick); a tick self-heal drops a leftover backdrop while the workspace is closed (`Ui: curtain self-heal`). With a fullscreen menu the top inset is −52 so the native top bar stays exposed.

Both sites use the same transparent click-blocker: color `(0, 0, 0, 0)`, still `raycastTarget = true`. Native in-game tabs (Spellbook, Vampire Powers) put a full-bleed `Image` on the hosted view under `FullscreenMenu.ViewParent` only; `FullscreenMenu` has no overlay `Image` of its own, so the world stays visible at the sides. Native title-screen sub-pages (`PlaySelectModeMenuView`) also have no overlay field. `ViewManager.GoToView` does **not** equal `SetActive(false)` on `MainMenuNewView`: it queues `NavigateToViewEvent`, `ViewLocationSystem` instantiates the destination prefab under `MainMenuCanvasBase.MenuParent` and, every frame, `UIHelper.SetActive`s `AmbienceVolumeGameObject` / `MusicVolumeGameObject` from `World.FindWorld(WorldType.Client)` (enum 4) plus `CurrentViewLocation` (the native `ViewLocationSystem.OnUpdate`). At the title that volume is already on for both MainMenu and PlaySelect, so it is not the Play-screen fog. Live gen6 two-state probe: cameras and scene roots are identical; the only background-bearing object on native Play (`ViewLocation.PlaySelect` = 17) is `PlaySelectMenuView_V2(Clone)/MenuBackgroundImage` (`Image` only — no `Animator`). Mods hides `MainMenu_V2` content children — `Content` (`SideBar/HomeViewInteractables`, news/DLC carousel, `LinkButtonSet`) and `SLS logo` — and leaves `BackgroundImage` (`VideoImage` / `StunVideoPlayer`) running so the castle video is never stopped; `SetActive(false)` on the view root is what left the title black. It does **not** call `GoToView` / `SetState` and does **not** paint a stand-in haze. While the workspace is up at `main-menu` it instantiates **only that child** (sprite + material + shader) as the first sibling of the backdrop canvas (`ModsCanvasBand.Backdrop` = 4998), stretched full-bleed under the workspace art (covering the still-playing title video). Acquisition is prefab-first: `MainMenuCanvasBase.MenuViews` `ViewMapping` for PlaySelect → `WeakAssetReference.GetAssetGuid` → `UIAssetSubSceneLoader_DefaultWorld.TryGetAsset<GameObject>` (the same call `ViewLocationSystem.OnUpdate` uses for `CurrentViewGUID`), then `Transform.Find("MenuBackgroundImage")`. Live-clone (a scene instance, or a generation-scoped template taken on the first Play visit) is the fallback; `source=none` means the fog is absent. The live Play view is never modified. Restoration of those content children **and** destruction of the clone (dead object tolerated; template + streaming token released on Uninstall so a Core swap cannot leak) is mandatory: workspace close, Escape, every Core unload reason, host destroy/recreate, and an exception mid-open. `mods_ui rects` reports `titleVideoPlaying` (`StunVideoPlayer.isPlaying`, or null). The owned MODS entry lives under `Content/SideBar/HomeViewInteractables` and returns at sibling 0 above CONTINUE when `Content` is shown again. Suspend does not change color; it only stretches the same graphic over the top bar. **Source-checked.** The cloned `Image` is the native Play fog; a lookalike is rejected.

The in-game tab site deactivates `FullscreenMenu.CurrentView.Instance` while Mods is open (the first half of the native `FullscreenMenu.SetState` — not an unmatched `SetState`, which would skip selecting a replacement and leave `_SelectedOuterTab` on Spellbook). The selected/hover pixels are not `OuterTabEntry.HighlightImage` alone: gen 18 drew red glyphs + underline while that Image was disabled and inactive. `SimpleStunSelectable.DoStateTransition` FontSwap (`DoFontSwap`, `FontState.highlightedFont` / `selectedFont`) and SpriteSwap (`DoSpriteSwap`, `SpriteState.highlightedSprite`) paint `ButtonTarget.GraphicTargets`; hover also toggles `StunHovereableButton.EnabledObjectsOnHover`. `ForceHighlight` is re-set from `SimpleStunButton.IsHovered` by `OnPointerEnter` / `Update` while the pointer rests on the exposed tab band, so while Mods is open the hold writes those painters back to Normal, sets `interactable=false` / `IsHovered=false` / `SelectableWhenNotInteractable=false` on the cached native buttons (restored on close), and re-applies on Bootstrap LateFrame after mapper `OnUpdate`. The owned MODS tab copies that selected painter (TMP font/material/colour and the native HighlightImage sprite at that image's rect) onto a child named `highlight`; close restores the unselected look and the previously selected native. The school-icon strip is `FullscreenMenu.InnerTabGroup`, a sibling of `ViewParent`, not hosted content — Spellbook fills it with seven `InnerTabEntry` icons; Vampire Powers and Social have no equivalent header. Clicking a native tab `Close()`s Mods then runs native `OnLeftClicked`, which SetState-replaces the hosted view. Restoration of the instance, the outer-tab visual and the header strip is the same set of paths as the title view.

**`mods_ui rects`.** `workspace-frame` is the resolved content rect (must match `native-background` within 8 canvas units on the in-game site when that row is found, else `native-container`). `rects` includes a `curtain` row `{present, active, raycastTarget, alpha, sortingOrder, x,y,width,height}` and `status.curtain` is `{present, raycastTarget}`. `backdrop` is the **clipped visible** rect (intersection with the frame, not the pre-mask Image) and carries `drawable` (assigned, active, enabled, alpha > 0, non-zero rect, mesh, canvas order below the panes). `native-container` is ViewParent when FullscreenMenu is up; `native-background` is the hosted page's `Background` / `BackgroundImage` painter (or the cached overhang applied to ViewParent); `found=false` with `why=` on a miss (title screen `title-screen-no-container` / `title-screen-ratio`, or the live read: `no-world` / `no-hud` / `no-fullscreen-menu` / `no-viewparent` / `no-overlay` / `degenerate` / `read-threw:…` / `no-hosted-view` / `no-background-image`). Detail also has `framePath`, `nativeFound`, `nativeWhy`, `nativeBackgroundWhy`, `backdropDrawable`, `blockerSorting` / `backdropSorting` / `panelsSorting` / `consentSorting` (the 5000 band), `titleViewHidden`, `hostedViewHidden`, `outerTabVisual` (`SpellBook:cleared` while the opening tab's underline is off), `sidebarAlpha`, `detailAlpha`, `nativeCanvasMax`, `playBackdrop` (`present`, `source` = `prefab` / `live-clone` / `none`, `sprite`, `material`, `shader` — the Play `MenuBackgroundImage` clone behind the title-site workspace; `source=live-clone` means the fog appears only after one Play visit this process). `nativeCanvasMax` walks ancestor names so an owned `overlay-N` canvas at Panels 4999 is not reported as native. The `main-menu` rect is found by walking `MainMenuNewView` (including inactive) so it remains listed after close.

**`mods_ui hit`.** Read-only same-Drain pointer probe. Invoke with `node tools/mcp/call.mjs client mods_ui '{"action":"hit","x":1360,"y":92}'` — `x`/`y` are client-area pixels, the same space as `rects`. Does not click, focus, or write Unity. `detail` is `{x, y, unityX, unityY, stripSlot, stripSlotWhy, stripRowHit, clickWouldInvoke, clickWouldInvokeWhy, hoverRow, focusField, focusFieldWhy, armedFields, uguiRaycast, uguiRaycastWhy}` plus `readback=same-drain`. `stripSlot` is what `ModsTabStrip.HitIndex` returns for that point on the live strip geometry (index, name, origin, per-slot rects); `stripRowHit` is every strip button whose `ScreenRectOf(row.Go)` contains the point, in row order, so overlap is visible; `clickWouldInvoke` is the button `ClickStripButtonAt` would invoke; `hoverRow.function` is `ResolveHoveredPanel` (the shown-input pump's hit name). `focusField` is what `FocusFieldAt` would pick (id, TMP rect, `readOnly` / `isFocused` / `interactable`, owner tab); `armedFields` is every armed field. `uguiRaycast` is the top `EventSystem.RaycastAll` hit (object path, canvas sorting order, `ours`). A missing field is `null` with a sibling `why` (`offline` / `no-strip` / `no-hit` / `no-event-system` / `read-threw:…`). Live invocations of `ClickStripButtonAt`, `FocusFieldAt`, and the held-tick visual drive also log `Ui: tabdiag …`. **Source-checked.** Not live-run.

**`mods_ui tabs`.** Read-only same-Drain native outer-tab visual probe. Invoke with `node tools/mcp/call.mjs client mods_ui '{"action":"tabs"}'`. Does not drive HighlightImage / ForceSelect. `detail` is `{native, owned, ownedWhy, nativeTabsLit, outerTabVisual, holdHeld, holdCleared, hold}` plus `readback=same-drain`; `hold` is `{held, cleared, tickRuns, lateRuns, lateSkippedWhy, painters, buttons, lastCaptureFrame, adoptedRows, hostedRestore}` (on demand; `hostedRestore` is `restored` / `skipped-different-page` / `none` from the native-pointer identity check). Each `native` / `owned` row is `{name, isSelected, highlightImage{enabled, activeSelf, activeInHierarchy, colourAlpha}, simpleStunButton{forceSelect, forceHighlight, selectionState}, tmp{colourAlpha}, graphics[], holdHeld, holdCleared, owned}`. `graphics` is an on-demand `GetComponentsInChildren<Graphic>(true)` walk (never per frame): `{path, type (Image|TMP), enabled, activeInHierarchy, rgba (four floats, 3 decimals), sprite}` and, for TMP, `fontAsset` + `material`. Owned `highlightImage` is the child named `highlight`, not the hit Image. `owned` is the MODS top-tab entry. `nativeTabsLit` and `outerTabVisual` match `rects`. **Source-checked.** Not live-run.

**`mods_ui scene`.** Read-only same-Drain probe of the title/Play view tree. It does not `SetActive` or `GoToView`. Invoke with `node tools/mcp/call.mjs client mods_ui '{"action":"scene"}'`. `detail` is `{view, viewWhy, ambience, music, titleView, menuParent, cameras, roots, stamp, playBackdrop}` plus `readback=same-drain`. `view` is `ViewManager.GetCurrentView()` as `{name, value}` or `null` with `viewWhy` (`offline`, `no-view-manager`, or `view-threw:…`). `ambience` / `music` are `MainMenuCanvasBase.AmbienceVolumeGameObject` / `MusicVolumeGameObject` (`present`, `activeSelf`, `activeInHierarchy`). `titleView` is `MainMenuNewView` including inactive: `present`, `activeSelf`, `videoImageEnabled`, and `video` (`StunVideoPlayer` / `UnityEngine.Video.VideoPlayer`: `isPlaying`, `isPrepared`, `enabled`). `menuParent.children` is a depth-first walk of `MainMenuCanvasBase.MenuParent` (MenuParent is depth 0, depth ≤ 6, cap 2048 objects, `truncated` when the cap is hit) with `path`, `activeSelf`, `activeInHierarchy`, and interesting `components` (Image, RawImage, Camera, ParticleSystem, VideoPlayer, Canvas, CanvasGroup with `canvasGroupAlpha`, SpriteRenderer, MeshRenderer, Volume, plus type names containing Fog / Smoke / Vignette / Ambience / Background / Particle / VFX / Video). Each `Image` / `RawImage` row also has `sprite`, `material` and `shader` names (empty string when unread) so a Mods-open clone can be matched to native Play's `MenuBackgroundImage`. `cameras` lists every scene Camera (`name`, `enabled`, `clearFlags`, `cullingMask`, `depth`, `hasTargetTexture`). `roots` are loaded-scene root names plus `activeSelf`. `stamp` is `{frame, width, height}` so two reads can be paired. `playBackdrop` is the same object as `rects.playBackdrop`. **Source-checked.** Not live-run.

**Input.** `WhileShown` unlocks the cursor and registers Core’s gameplay block (order 599) for the whole time a pane is shown, not only while hovered. That is how Escape is consumed. Details: [ui.md](ui.md#input-what-a-panel-takes-and-for-how-long).

## Modal policy

**Removed 2026-10-05: the consent prompt and the workspace suspension it caused.** Servers no longer send mod files ([client-mod-policy.md](client-mod-policy.md#security)), so Core has no consent prompt, the Sample's demo offer (`SampleMods.TryOfferDemoPackage`, `SampleConsentDemo`, `.consentdemo`) is gone, and nothing suspends an open Mods workspace any more. Source-checked: `ModsMenu.IsSuspended`, `SuspendedChanged` and the `suspended` / `suspendReason` readback remain, but no code sets the suspension now, so `suspended` reads `false`. `mods_ui` no longer reports `consentPromptOpen` or `occludedBy=consent`. The earlier behavior (suspend, close-on-empty, Escape as Decline, the action-row layout) is in git history (`docs/mods-menu.md` at `7a055b42`); its live evidence below is dated.

`mods_ui` status: `suspended` (`false`), `suspendReason` (null), `tab` (`mods` / `install` / `configuration` / `keybinds` while open, otherwise `null`). `select_tab` takes `args.tab` = `mods` | `install` | `configuration` | `keybinds`, requires an open workspace, never calls `Close()`, and reads back on the next Drain (`NeedsNextDrain`). `select` (plugin page) is Mods-tab only; a catalog row is not `OpenMod`. Every open, close, hide, select, page change and sidebar rebuild logs `Mods workspace: <transition> from=… to=… reason=<user-button|shortcut|shortcut:<key>|reattach|settle|native-tab|mods_ui|lifecycle:<reason>|inventory-refresh|unknown> caller=<method>` (identical repeats suppressed). A sidebar rebuild (search or inventory refresh) keeps the current plugin and page. The owned Mods button passes `user-button` through NativeModsEntry’s stored open delegate; the F10 (rebindable) shortcut logs `shortcut:<key>`; `mods_ui` still clicks that button, so it shows as `user-button` / `caller=NativeModsEntry` rather than `mods_ui`. Tick, settle and re-attach do not invoke the stored open — they close or ignore.

The click-eating curtain is owned by `NativeModsEntry`. `Ui.Panel` overlays (dialogs, the deny panel) sit at sorting order 5000, above blocker 4997 / backdrop 4998 / panels 4999, and keep their own GraphicRaycaster and capture. Do not call `SetState` on the workspace's own transitions — that would destroy/activate hosted views and play UI audio a second time.

**Selected-tab state.** Opening Mods from the fullscreen HUD does **not** `SetState(System)` and does **not** `SetState` an unmatched value. In the game's metadata `FullscreenMenuState` is Emotes=0 … System=6; this HUD lists all seven as outer tabs. The native `SetState` deactivates `CurrentView.Instance` then matches `Views[i].State`; unmatched returns after the destroy with `_SelectedOuterTab` unchanged. Mods does that first half only: `SetActive(false)` on the hosted instance, then clears the opening tab. The selected/hover pixels are ColorTint on three painters under the entry — `Glow` (Glow04, `Colors.selectedColor.a` ~0.5), `Name` TMP (white), `Name/Line` (OuterTab_UnderLine, alpha 1) — via `SimpleStunSelectable.StartColorTween` → `Graphic.CrossFadeColor` writing `CanvasRenderer.SetColor`. `HighlightImage` is the unused new-unlock star. The held snap set is those three paths only (`NativeTabSelection.IsHeldPainterPath`); FIX6 wrote `Colors.normalColor` into the sprite-less root Graphic's CanvasRenderer (`ApplyHeldPainters` → `SetColor`) and left an opaque white box open and after close. Capture records `graphic.color` / renderer colour/alpha before the first clear so hold can snap idle (Glow/Line renderer alpha 0, Name 0.604 gray), not `Colors.normalColor` when that is opaque white. Release does **not** write those captures back: the game ColorTints a tab only on that button's own transitions, so a stale Map/Social capture (gen 28) stayed lit through later `native_tab` switches. On close, every native is written canonical idle (Glow 0, Name 0.604, Line 0) except the selected page (`FullscreenMenu.CurrentView.State` / mapper `_SelectedOuterTab`, not the unreliable entry `IsSelected`) which gets the selected ColorTint (`ButtonTarget.Colors.selectedColor` per painter, fallback Glow 0.502 / Name white 0.961 / Line 1) via `SetColor`/`SetAlpha`/`CrossFadeAlpha(…,0,true)`, then `InitInstantHighlight`/`Refresh` on that tab as before. A client already stuck (Map/Social lit) self-heals on the next close. `mods_ui tabs` `isSelected` is true for that selected native when closed; `detail.selectedOuterTab` names it. One `Ui: tabdiag release selected=<name> idle=<n> lit-after=<n>` line is logged on release. Open hold is unchanged (natives idle, only MODS lit). A one-shot gen-22 repair at hold capture zeroes renderer alpha / `crossFadeAlpha` on a sprite-less root whose renderer alpha is 1 (pre-FIX6 / Glow-Line idle), and is not on the per-frame path. `ForceHighlight` is re-set from `SimpleStunButton.IsHovered` by `OnPointerEnter` / `Update` even when `interactable` is false, so the hover lock is root `raycastTarget=false` (restored on close) plus `ForceHighlight=false` every late pass — `interactable=false` is not re-applied (mapper Refresh restores it; a late Disabled transition would tint the root). InnerTabGroup (the Spellbook school strip; empty on Vampire Powers / Social) is hidden with the same restore guarantees as `HostedViewHold`. The owned MODS tab is the last sibling of `FullscreenMenu.OuterTabGroup` (`HorizontalLayoutGroup`): it copies a native entry's `LayoutElement` sizes (and `sizeDelta` height) only — never `anchoredPosition` / anchors / pivot, which is the gen-24 overlap with Emotes. It copies that native selected triple — Glow04 child `glow` (native Glow RectTransform relative to the entry, sibling behind caption), OuterTab_UnderLine child `highlight` (native `Name/Line` anchors/pivot/`anchoredPosition`/`sizeDelta` relative to `Name`, parented under the caption as sibling 0), white caption with the native font/material — then re-applies the clones once after `LayoutRebuilder.ForceRebuildLayoutImmediate` on the group (and once on the next Tick if the first pass ran before layout); unselected is Glow 0 / Line 0 / caption 0.604 gray; never copies `Stunlock_Icon_NewStar`. Show writes opaque-white `Image.color` plus CanvasRenderer colour/alpha and an instant `CrossFadeAlpha` (Glow 0.502, underline 1, from the native selected ColorTint captured at attach; hide zeroes the renderer and deactivates) because ColorTint paints through the renderer — a `Graphic.color` write alone stays invisible at inherited renderer alpha 0. `mods_ui tabs` rows add `interactable` / `isHovered` / `raycastTarget`; each `graphics` row adds `crossFadeAlpha` / `canvasRendererColor` / `rect` `{x,y,w,h}` (screen px via `TransformPoint` of two corners) / `siblingIndex` / local RectTransform `anchoredPosition` / `sizeDelta` / `anchorMin` / `anchorMax` / `pivot` `{x,y}` (on demand). `mods_ui rects` reports `outerTabVisual` (`SpellBook:cleared` while that tab's underline is off, or `none:cleared` when no native page was selected — never the Emotes default) only when `nativeTabsLit` is 0. `native_tab same=true` still clicks the captured opening tab. Close / Escape / unload / world-end / exception mid-open restore the visual, the header strip, canonical idle/selected painters plus captured `raycastTarget` / interactable flags / HighlightImage / hover objects, and the held instance (dead object tolerated); a native tab click still wins because Mods closes and native `OnLeftClicked` runs SetState. A blank FullscreenMenu after `GoToMenu` from HUD None still `SetState(System)` so a hosted view exists — that is attach, then Mods hides it. **Source-checked.** Next live capture: open unchanged from gen 28 (only MODS lit, natives dark); after close only the selected native is lit (System when Mods opened from System), Map/Social dark, MODS gray with nothing under it; `tabs` `isSelected` true for exactly one native and `detail.selectedOuterTab` names it.

**Hold vs release.** While Mods is open the hold owns native outer-tab visuals: late-frame idle snap on Glow/Name/Line through `canvasRenderer.SetColor`/`SetAlpha` and instant `CrossFadeColor`/`CrossFadeAlpha` only (never base `Graphic.color`), `ForceSelect`/`ForceHighlight` off, `HighlightImage` off, hosted page hidden. Root `raycastTarget` stays on so a real click still reaches the owned `EventTrigger` and closes Mods onto that page; hover stays dark because the late pass clears `ForceHighlight`/`IsHovered` and snaps the three painters idle. Close stops those late writes first, then removes only those overrides (restore captured `Graphic.enabled`, `interactable` / `SelectableWhenNotInteractable` — not `raycastTarget` or base color) and calls each entry’s `Refresh(isSelected: entry.State == CurrentView.State, requirementMet, highlight: false)` so the mapper owns subsequent frames. On hold capture, a Glow/Line/root whose `Graphic.color` is a value this integration once wrote (`[1,1,1,0]` on Glow or Line, white on Glow, opaque white on the sprite-less root) is repaired once to the pristine base (Glow `[0.502,0,0.094,1]`, Line `[1,1,1,1]`, root `[1,1,1,0]`), logged `Ui: tabdiag repaired-base <path> <from> -> <to>`. Those restore fields are process-lifetime plain rows keyed by tab name (no object references) so a Core swap while Mods is open adopts them instead of re-capturing the written values. Release never paints a saved selection, never sets `ForceSelect` from the page bit, never calls `InitInstantHighlight`, and never writes `HighlightImage`; a native-tab click that closed Mods keeps that click’s view. One `Ui: tabdiag release selected=<name> idle=<n> lit-after=<n> forceSelectCleared=<n>` line is logged on success; a dead host or exception still clears ownership and logs a warning with the reason.

## Register a page (`ctx.Menu`)

Call from `ProgenitorPlugin<TContext>.OnLoad`. `ctx.Menu.Register`, `AllowServerEdits` and `ctx.Settings.Page` are tracked for every unload reason. Register once per plugin. Settings are existing `ConfigEntry` objects; their types, descriptions and acceptable values remain authoritative. Lay them out with the settings page builder (`ctx.Settings.Page`, SETTINGS1 stage 2, [mod-settings.md](mod-settings.md#the-settings-page-builder-stage-2)); `.Settings(title, entries…)` groups still work and are drawn after the page's sections.

```csharp
var hints = ctx.Bind("Menu", "ShowHints", true, "Show the help text on the Example page.");
var limit = ctx.Bind("Menu", "ExampleLimit", 10, "Demonstration limit; does not change combat.",
    synced: true, acceptableValues: new AcceptableValueRange<int>(1, 20));
// … HintStyle, HintTopics, Notes and ExperimentalLayout, bound the same way …
var notice = ctx.Bind("Menu", "ServerNotice", "", "A notice for everyone on the server (multi-line text).",
    synced: true);
ctx.Settings.Page(page => page
    .Section("Personal")
        .Toggle(hints, help: "Show the help text on the Example page.")
        .Radio(style, showIf: () => hints.Value)
        .MultiSelect(topics, choices: new[] { "Combat", "Travel", "Building" }, showIf: () => hints.Value)
        .Text(notes, multiline: true, lines: 3)
        .Toggle(layout, needsRestart: true)
    .Section("Server")
        .Slider(limit, scope: SettingScope.Server, help: "Demonstration limit; the server's value wins while joined.")
        .Text(notice, multiline: true, lines: 4, scope: SettingScope.Server, label: "Server notice",
            help: "Shown to everyone on the server; an administrator edits it.")
        .MaxLength(1000)
    .Section("Demo")
        .Info("Local time", () => DateTime.Now.ToString("HH:mm:ss", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
        .Divider()
        .Button("Log a line", () => ctx.Log.LogInfo("Sample settings: button confirmed and run."),
            confirm: "Write a line to the Sample log?", help: "A confirmed action: it asks first."));

ctx.Menu.Register("Progenitor Sample", "A working example of settings and custom mod pages.")
    .Page("Example", panel =>
    {
        panel.Label("Custom pages share Progenitor's controls and cleanup.");
        if (hints.Value) panel.Label("Change ShowHints under Personal, then reopen this page.");
        panel.Label("Server example limit: " + limit.Value);
        var status = panel.Label("Ready.");
        panel.Button("Try an action").OnClick(() =>
        {
            status.Text = "Action handled by this generation of the Sample.";
            ctx.Log.LogInfo("Sample menu: action handled.");
        });
    });
```

The Sample owns one custom page, a settings page with every stage 2 control, and one synced setting that its `scope: SettingScope.Server` **explicitly** opts into server editing. Do not copy this snippet into a second source file; compile against `SampleMenu.cs`.

Build custom pages with the supplied panel only. Progenitor disposes its widgets and callbacks when the page closes or the owning registration unloads. Track other resources with `ctx.Track`.

`.Header(panel => …)` (SETTINGS1) replaces the default title and by-line of the mod's page with the mod's own rows. It runs after the framework's status line is drawn and gets a row-only panel, so it cannot change that line or hide, retitle or dispose the page; a builder that throws has its rows removed and the default is drawn. The settings of every `.Settings(title, …)` group are drawn on the mod page as a section; see [mod-settings.md](mod-settings.md).

## Settings rows

On the mod page the rows are drawn inline in sections, one grid row per setting, with typed controls (toggle, dropdown or segmented picker, radio, multi-select, number, slider with an exact field, text and multi-line text, color, keybind), a per-row reset in the row's gutter and **Reset all**; the control rules and readback are in [mod-settings.md](mod-settings.md). The admin path below is the same on the page and in the pane. The table describes the separate settings pane (`SettingsPanel`, reached through `mods_ui select page=settings` and `SettingsPanel.Open()`).

Classification is `SettingsRow.ForEntry`: synced or foreign entries start as `ReadOnly`. The panel then:

| Kind | When | Widget | Write |
|---|---|---|---|
| Personal | Not the admin path; bool / number / string / enum / keybind | Toggle, input, or rebind | `Commit` → `TryWrite` through the `ConfigEntry`. BepInEx saves the cfg. |
| Synced, read-only | Synced and `ServerSettings.CanRequest` is false | Label only | Suffix `(set by the server)` if ConfigSync has locked the row, else `(awaiting server synchronization)`. `TryWrite` refuses: the server owns the value for this session. The client never writes a server value into the personal cfg. |
| Opted-in admin | `row.IsSynced && ServerSettings.CanRequest(row.RowId)` | Input + **Apply …** + status note | `ServerSettings.Request(rowId, typed, displayedValue, …)`. No local `TryWrite`. |

`CanRequest` requires a live `ServerSettings` registration **and** a ConfigSync lock. Bind with `synced: true` and call `ctx.Menu.AllowServerEdits(entry)` on **both sides**, as the Sample does. The setting must already be registered with ConfigSync. A compatible completed handshake is required. Changing other synchronized settings through this screen remains unsupported. Foreign (chainloader-discovered) rows stay read-only unless `[Settings] EditOtherModsSettings = true`.

The editor sends the value the player originally saw. The server re-reads the sender's `User` from the connection and authorizes it like a remote install: a SteamID on the server's admin list (no `adminauth` needed), else `User.IsAdmin` (`ServerAdminList.AuthorizeShared`, the reader remote install uses; with no readable list only `User.IsAdmin` counts). Each sender may make 10 requests per 60 seconds, refused ones included; past that the answer is `Too many changes. Wait a moment and try again.` The server then checks handshake, peer inventory, exact opt-in, type, expected-value conflict, parse and bounds, and applies through ordinary ConfigSync. Every decided request is audited: a `ServerSettings audit:` line in the BepInEx log and the same line in `BepInEx/progenitor-settings.log` (rolled to `.1` past 1 MiB), for example `2026-10-03T12:00:00Z settings accepted by Vlad (76561198000000001, adminlist) example.mod/General/Count "3" -> "5"`; a refusal adds `: <reason>`, and a rate-limited sender is written once per window. The admin-list authorization, the limit and the audit are game-free tested (`ServerSettingsAdminParityTests`) and **not live-verified**.

**Admin-list acceptance procedure (run live 2026-10-04; results below).** On the isolated test server only, with a Core containing this change on both sides and the Sample (whose `Menu/ExampleLimit` is opted in):

**Live (2026-10-04, Steam client in game on the isolated test server, both sides on branch fix/settings-admin-parity f43dfe56):** (1) on the adminlist without `adminauth`, applying Example limit 10 → 12 logged `settings accepted by TestPlayer (76561198000000076, adminlist) …/Menu/ExampleLimit "10" -> "12"` in the server log and `BepInEx/progenitor-settings.log`; (2) with the adminlist emptied, 12 → 13 was refused `(…, none) …: Only server administrators can change this setting.`; after restoring the list, running `adminauth` and emptying the list again, 12 → 14 was accepted `(…, adminauth)`; (3) twelve applies within a minute: the first ten were refused on bounds (`Outside the values this setting accepts.`, values above the range) and still counted, the eleventh answered `Too many changes. Wait a moment and try again.`, and the twelfth wrote no further audit line; (4) the adminlist was restored and the value set back to 10 (accepted, `adminlist`).

1. Put the test player's SteamID in the test server's `adminlist.txt` and join without running `adminauth`. On the Sample's page apply a new **Example limit**. Expect `Saved by the server.`, the server log line `ServerSettings audit: … settings accepted by <name> (<steamid>, adminlist) progenitor.sample/Menu/ExampleLimit "<old>" -> "<new>"`, and the same line in the server's `BepInEx/progenitor-settings.log`.
2. Remove the SteamID from `adminlist.txt` (the list is re-read on change) and apply again. Expect the refusal `Only server administrators can change this setting.` on the row and a `refused … (…, none) …` audit line. Run `adminauth`, apply again, and expect it to be accepted with `adminauth` in the audit line.
3. Apply 11 changes within a minute. Expect the eleventh to answer `Too many changes. Wait a moment and try again.` and only one rate-limited audit line.
4. Restore `adminlist.txt` and the setting's value, disconnect, and leave the client at the title screen.

The row reports pending (`Waiting for the server…`), accepted (`Saved by the server.`), refused (the server error text) or timed out (8 seconds: `No server confirmation arrived. Check the current value before retrying.`). A second Apply on the same row while one request is pending is refused. Closing the page disposes the request handle and forgets the callback; it cannot retract a change already sent. After a timeout or conflict (`This setting changed on the server. Review the current value and try again.`), reopen and read the current value before retrying.

After a client-only Core swap, both sides need the updated Core so the handshake refresh can restore server config locks. Recovery waits approximately 5.1 seconds before requesting the ordinary handshake; uninterrupted locking across that interval is not claimed.

`ServerSettings.Register` and `ServerSettings.Request` are the lower-level APIs. Track their disposable handles when using them directly. `ServerSettingResult` contains `Success`, `Value` and `Error`. The request overload that takes `expectedValue` is the UI editor path; capture the displayed value.

## Landing page (LANDING1, 2026-10-10)

Opening the Mods tab without a mod selected shows Progenitor's landing page (`ModsMenu.Open` → `BuildLanding`; texts and the tile rows are the game-free `ModsLanding`, user-approved mockup):

- Title **Progenitor — Mods**; subtitle `Your mods, in one place.` followed by the local and the server summary, each condensed to its first sentence with its qualifiers shortened in parentheses, never dropped (`7 mods found on this client (1 more reported, loading unconfirmed; list partial) · server mod list unavailable (known requirements shown)`, `ModsLanding.Condense`).
- Intro `Progenitor is a mod manager and mod framework for V Rising.` and four short points (installs and updates, its own client and server features, coordinating the mods built on it, a framework for mod authors): a left-aligned block 60% of the pane wide, centered as a block, each point behind a `•` (U+2022, drawn as a glyph cell: the middle dot `·` when the font lacks it; live the middle dot read as a speck) in a column of its own (a wrapped point hangs under its first word), muted at 17 px against the intro's 20 (`ModsLanding.PointRow`, rows `landing-point-1`..`4`).
- A 2×2 grid of bordered tiles (the kit's [tiles](ui.md#bordered-tiles-tiles1)), equally wide, each with the same native-styled kit button (the key cap; no underlined links, requested 2026-10-10) on the first line and a muted note on the second: **Your settings** (Progenitor's page selected exactly as its left-list row selects it: the **Required by Progenitor** group opened, the row highlighted, the row's own `OpenMod` path, and the row scrolled into view; `OpenProgenitorFromLanding`), **Server settings** (Progenitor on this server for an admin there, else the Server tab with the note `Join a server as its admin to change these`), **Preferences** (the Preferences tab, as its strip button selects it) and **Collect logs** (`SupportActions.Collect`, the same collect as Get help). Collect logs' second line is `Collect the logs for a help post to assist with troubleshooting a mod.`; while a collect runs it is the collect's progress, then the outcome's first line for 10 s, then the description again (`ModsLanding.CollectNote`), re-read every second while the row is visible (a hidden page keeps its clock and reads nothing). The newest bundle stays on Progenitor's **Get help** page (**Last bundle**). The newest-bundle lookup behind Get help's **Last bundle** is cached for 10 s, keyed by the two folders (so a changed **Save bundles in** reads again at once) and cleared when a collect finishes (`SupportActions.NewestLine`).
- **Help guide** (game-free tested, not live-verified): a third tile line, drawn only while Progenitor's guide is installed (`BepInEx/progenitor-core/docs/guide/progenitor-guide.html`, else `BepInEx/progenitor-core/help/index.html`; `ModsMenu.ProgenitorGuideTarget`). Its one tile, note `Guides for players, server admins and mod developers`, takes the left slot with the right slot empty (`CellTiles.Single`), so it is exactly as wide as, and lines up under, Preferences: a 2×3 grid with one empty slot. Pressing it opens the guide in the browser as a `file://` URI, the same opener as a mod page's [Help button](mod-settings.md#help-button). Without the guide the grid stays 2×2.
- Footer: `Choose a mod on the left for its status, settings and pages. Install and update mods on the Install mods tab.`, a centered row.
- Spacing: `ModsLanding.BlockGap` (14 units) above the first tile line and above the footer, `CellTiles.LineGap` (14) between the two tile lines (live the footer touched the last tiles).

**Readback.** `mods_ui` content rows carry `landing`: `landing-subtitle`, `landing-intro`, `landing-point-1`..`landing-point-4` (each with its text), `landing-tiles-top`, `landing-tiles-top-notes`, `landing-tiles-bottom`, `landing-last-bundle` (the bottom notes row, key `landing-tiles-bottom-notes`, with the Collect logs note; the readback key is kept from the round that showed the Last bundle line there), with the guide installed `landing-tiles-help` (`tiles` with target `help-guide`, and `file`, the guide's path under `progenitor-core`) and `landing-tiles-help-notes` (cells `help-guide`, `help-guide-note`, tile 5, and the untiled `empty-slot` cells), `landing-footer`; the tiles' cells are `your-settings`, `server-settings`, `preferences` and `collect-logs` (each with a `-note` cell on the notes row), every tile cell reads back its `tile` (1..4) and each tile row its `tileEdge` (`first` on the link rows, `last` on the notes rows). `widget_click label=<caption>` clicks a tile focus-free (`Your settings`, `Server settings`, `Preferences`, `Collect logs`; `Help guide` also works but opens the browser, which takes the player's focus). Each tile is one place in the back/forward history: Back (the `nav_back` bind, the mouse's Back button) returns to the landing and Forward to the tile's target (`ThreeHomesMenuTests.Back_after_a_landing_tile_returns_to_the_landing_and_Forward_to_the_tile`); `mods_ui nav_state` shows the steps, `current`, and `sidebarReveal` (`plugin`, `found`, `selected`, `measured`, `rowTop`/`viewportTop`/`viewportHeight`) for Your settings' left-list row.

Status: first version live-checked on the Steam client at the title, 5120×1440 (2026-10-10): the points read as a ragged centered column and the tiles as loose links, so this round reworked both. The rework is source-checked, built and game-free tested (`ThreeHomesMenuTests` landing tests, `SupportLandingAndTileTests`: equal tile widths at 1248, 1108 and 700 px, matching head and note boxes, the centered points block with one bullet column), not live-verified. The second round (bordered tiles, the points block, the drawn title tile) was live-checked at 5120×1440 on 2026-10-10; the block spacing, bullet and title result line were live-checked after that. The key-cap tiles, the Collect logs description, Your settings' left-list selection, the landing's Back/Forward and the centered title line are source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified.

## Get help: collect logs (SUPPORT1)

Players report problems in the **#technical-support** forum of the V Rising Mod Community Discord (or on Thunderstore). That server accepts attachments up to 50 MB, so the logs go out as one zip of at most 45 MB.

**Player steps.** If the game crashed, start it again; then Mods → Progenitor → **Get help** → **Collect logs** (title screen or in a world). The row's status reads `Done.` and **Last bundle** reads `Collecting logs...`, then `Saved vrising-mod-logs-client-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>.zip (1.4 MB) and opened its folder; its path is on the clipboard. Drag it into your #technical-support post.` Explorer opens with the zip selected (`explorer.exe /select,"<path>"`, Windows only; without it the line names the folder instead: `on your Desktop`, `in <folder>`).

**Where it is saved.** On the Desktop (`Environment.SpecialFolder.DesktopDirectory`) by default, or in the folder of **Save bundles in** (`[Support] OutputFolder` in `progenitor.ui.cfg`, Your settings · Get help; empty is the Desktop; quotes and `%VAR%` are accepted; a missing folder is created, the Desktop never is). On many PCs the Desktop is synced by OneDrive, so the zip is uploaded to the player's cloud too; it is already redacted, and the setting's help says so. When the Desktop is not found, does not exist, or the folder cannot be written, the zip goes to `BepInEx/progenitor-support` and the line says why: `Saved <name> (1.4 MB) in BepInEx/progenitor-support because your Desktop does not exist and opened its folder; ...`. Only `BepInEx/progenitor-support` keeps its five newest bundles; nothing on the Desktop or in a chosen folder is ever deleted. **Open folder** opens the folder bundles are saved to when it exists, else `BepInEx/progenitor-support` (created if needed). Before any collect in this session, **Last bundle** shows the newest zip in either folder (name, size, local time) or `None yet.` The dedicated server's `.progenitor logs` always saves to its own `BepInEx/progenitor-support`.

**How to ask for help, inside the zip.** README.txt opens with **HOW TO ASK FOR HELP**: open the #technical-support forum of the V Rising Mod Community Discord and click New Post (`Join the Discord: https://vrisingmods.com/discord`, then `Already in the server? Open #technical-support: <channel link>`); the title `<mod> – <what went wrong>`, prefilled with the mod the logs point at most (else `Unknown mod`); what to write (what you were doing, what happened, when it started and after which mod's install or update, every time or not, client or server, single player / private game / dedicated server); attach the zip (under 50 MB); check the mod's Thunderstore page and mention its author. summary.txt starts with the same in two lines. No Discord link is printed until `SupportBundle.DiscordInviteUrl` is set (empty now).

**Likely involved (SupportDiagnosis).** summary.txt's next section reads the bundle's own redacted logs and names at most three mods, `Likely involved (from the logs; not proof):`, each with a score, counts and up to three sample lines; with thin evidence it says `No mod stands out.` The status line (and the `.progenitor logs` reply) ends with `Likely involved: <mod> (from the logs; not proof).` when a mod stands out. Evidence and points: BepInEx `[Error  :<Source>]` lines (3) and `[Fatal  :<Source>]` lines (5) whose source is a loaded plugin's name or GUID; an exception (4) by its first stack frame in a plugin's namespace (types' namespaces of each plugin's assembly, read on the main thread; Core's count for Progenitor's own entry; `System`, `BepInEx`, `Il2Cpp*`, `Unity*`, `ProjectM`, `Stunlock`, `HarmonyLib`, `MonoMod` and similar frames are skipped, and a namespace two plugins share points at neither); Harmony patch failures and conflicts (4) naming a plugin; BepInEx load failures and missing or incompatible dependencies (5, by the `[Name 1.2.3]` in the line, so a plugin that never loaded is named too); Progenitor's mod problems (3); and the previous session: a Unity `Crash!!!` block in `Player-prev.log` whose native frames name a plugin assembly (5), and when that log lacks Unity's clean-quit `Memory Statistics` dump, the last three plugins to write to `session-1.log` (2 each, with a line saying the previous session did not end normally or crashed). Each piece of evidence is grouped by mod, kind and **signature** (the line with numbers, hex, entity ids, times and short arguments in parentheses taken out, so `GetSystemTypeIndex(<any type>) threw` is one signature), and repeats of one signature give diminishing returns (points × (1 + log2 count), at most × 4), so one error repeated thousands of times cannot outweigh several different ones. **Baseline noise:** a signature that also appears in a session that ended normally (the previous session, when `Player-prev.log` has the clean-quit marker), or in every session collected (this one, `session-1.log`, `session-2.log`) when none of them is known to have ended badly, weighs × 0.1 and its sample lines end `(also in sessions that ended normally)`; with only such noise the section says `No mod stands out.` and README.txt suggests `Unknown mod`. Evidence from a previous session that did not end normally weighs × 1.5, and × 2 in its last 200 lines. `LogOutput.log` and `progenitor-logs/session.log` of one process (this one, `hosted-server/`, `local-server-<n>/`) hold the same session, so only `session.log` is read when the bundle has it. The plugin list covers the chainloader's plugins and the ones loaded from `BepInEx/progenitor-reload` (every `[BepInPlugin]` type in a collectible assembly other than Core). Framework frames that only host or dispatch to plugins (`Progenitor.Plugins.*`, `Progenitor.Reload.*`, hooks, events, systems, commands, the main-thread queue) give way to a deeper plugin frame in the same trace; only a trace with no other plugin frame points at Progenitor. A mod is listed from 6 points, and only when its evidence that is not noise reaches 6 on its own: many different background errors add up, but noise alone never names a mod (live 2026-10-10, Steam client: four startup errors per session scored 13 in total and named Progenitor before this rule). Progenitor is judged like any other mod and is named `Progenitor` (not its BepInEx plugin name, Progenitor Bootstrap) in the section, the README title and the status line. One pass per log, line by line: 16 MB in well under a second.

**What the zip holds.** `README.txt` (how to ask for help, what this is, what was removed, review before posting); `summary.txt` (UTC time, side, OS, .NET runtime, Core / Bootstrap / Dev versions, game build, BepInEx version, whether in a world and hosting, the BepInEx plugins with GUID, name and version, the reloadable plugins, the handshake's mod list, current mod problems, the files in `BepInEx/plugins`, `patchers`, `progenitor-reload` and `progenitor-disabled` by relative path, size and date, the files in the zip and any file skipped with its reason); `mods.md` (each loaded mod, chainloader and `progenitor-reload` plugins: name, id, version, author (the package's Thunderstore team, from this side's `progenitor-install.json` row, else a package `manifest.json` beside the DLL), whether it is built on Progenitor, its Thunderstore page, the manifest's `website_url` (http(s) only; no user info, query string or the template's placeholder), its help page and its BepInEx folder; on a joined client also the server's join-list mods, id and version only; never a file path; names and versions on one line; each local mod's id and name are kept from player-name redaction unless a player has that name, authors are not; if the list cannot be written the bundle still is and summary.txt says why); `bepinex/` with `LogOutput.log`, any `LogOutput.*.log` / `LogOutput.log.prev` written in the last day, `progenitor-logs/session*.log`, `ErrorLog.log` (Steam's), the audit logs `progenitor-settings.log`, `progenitor-remote-install.log`, `progenitor-auto-update.log` (and their `.1`), `progenitor-install.json`, `progenitor-mod-state.json`, `progenitor-auto-update.json`, `progenitor-self-install.json`, `progenitor-plugin-actions.json`, `progenitor-versions/progenitor/self-update.json`, `config/BepInEx.cfg` and Progenitor's own cfg files (chat and message values hidden, below); `unity/` with `Player*.log` from `%USERPROFILE%\AppData\LocalLow\Stunlock Studios\VRising` (the client's `Player.log` and `Player-prev.log`; on the development PC the same folder also holds `Player-server.log` and `Player-server-prev.log`, which the hosted private game writes); and `hosted-server/bepinex/` with the hosted server's `LogOutput.log`, `LogOutput.log.prev`, `ErrorLog.log` and session logs while this client hosts a private game, or when the game folder's `VRising_Server/BepInEx/LogOutput.log` was written in the last day; and `local-server-<n>/` (at most three) for each dedicated server running on this PC (below). Each log keeps its last 16 MB from a line start, after a `[truncated: first N bytes omitted]` line; when the zip comes out over 45 MB it is rebuilt with half the cap (down to 128 KB). A state file is cut at 1 MB.

Status of `mods.md`: source-checked, built and game-free tested (`SupportModListTests`: ledger and manifest packages, links, help page, URL filter, order, redaction in the zip), 2026-10-17. Not live-verified.

**Never collected:** chat history (`progenitor-cache/chat`), `progenitor-server-profiles.json`, `progenitor-last-server.json`, `progenitor-server-requirements.json` (it names every server this client joined, by host), `progenitor-refused-joins.json`, `progenitor-trusted-operators.json`, `ServerHostSettings.json`, `ServerGameSettings.json`, the admin and ban lists, saves, `*.token` and `*.key` files, and other mods' configs. The rule is applied to each file's path inside the zip, which is relative to its root (`bepinex/`, `hosted-server/bepinex/`, `unity/`), so folders above the game (a game under `D:\Saves\`) never exclude a log. In the cfg files that are collected, the values of `MutedPlayers`, `MentionWords`, `TestNames`, `Tabs` and `Colors` (`[Chat]` in `progenitor.ui.cfg`), `Message` and `NoProgenitorMessage` (the server message) and `Description` and `Published` (the server listing) read `[hidden]`; an empty value stays empty.

**Redaction** (`SupportRedactor`, one instance per bundle so numbering is consistent across files): SteamID64s → `[steam-1]`, `[steam-2]`, and the same accounts in their other spellings — the game's `{Steam <account id>}`, SteamID3 `[U:1:<account id>]` and `STEAM_0:<y>:<z>` — take the SteamID64's number (`0x0110000100000000 + account id`), or a number of their own when they don't map; IPv4 addresses → `[ip-1]` except `127.*`, `0.*`, `255.*` and a dotted quad right after `v`, `ver`, `version` or `build` (`Version=1.0.0.0` stays; numbers above 255 or a fifth part are never addresses); non-loopback IPv6 → `[ip-N]`; host names in `steamipv4://` / `steamipv6://` connect strings → `[host-1]` (`localhost` stays); the Windows profile folder → `%USERPROFILE%`; the user and computer names → `[user]`, `[machine]`, as a whole word when 5+ characters long and only as a path segment (after `\` or `/`) when 3–4, and never when the name is a common word (`admin`, `Administrator`, `server`, `steam`, `user`, `desktop` and similar); Discord webhook paths → `/api/webhooks/[hidden]`; email addresses → `[email]`. Secret values → `[hidden]`, for a name whose last dotted word ends in token, password, secret, an API, private or access key, a webhook URL or a credential, or is `key`, `pass` or `auth` (`TokenFile` is not secret): in cfg lines (the rest of the line), `key=value`, `key: value`, JSON and query strings (quotes kept, an empty `""` kept: `ServerHostSettings.Password="[hidden]"`, `GameConnect.ConnectPassword=""`), the game's `'Password' (...) overridden by Command Line Parameter '-password' - New value: [hidden]` lines, and `-password x` / `-rconPassword "x"` arguments in logged command lines. Every pattern is linear in its input and carries a 5 s match timeout; a file whose redaction still times out is replaced whole by a `[withheld: redaction timed out ...]` line and marked in summary.txt, never shipped unredacted. Error and status text shown to the player or sent to the admin is redacted the same way. Player names → `[player-1]`, `[player-2]` (whole word, case-insensitive, the same number in every file): the names the game knows, read on the main thread (on a client every `User` in the client world, this player included; on a server every `User`, offline ones included, for both `.progenitor logs` and `'PrSL'`), plus the names a first pass learns from the collected logs' known formats — the game's `PersonaName: <name>,` Steam line, `admin="<name>"` (`progenitor-remote-install.log`), `settings accepted|refused by <name> (` (`progenitor-settings.log`), and Progenitor's server lines that name a player (`Debug build: on by <name> (`, `PhotoMode: <name> asked to`, `report ... from <name> (`, `unit look(s) to <name>.`, `) to <name>: sent=`). Names shorter than 3 characters, digits only, common words, log words (`Error`, `Server`, `Hidden` and the like), common game words (`Ghost`, `Night`, `Blood`, `Vampire`, `Castle` and the like), placeholder labels (`a new player`, anything starting with `a `/`an `/`the ` or holding digits in parentheses), and any loaded plugin's name, GUID, GUID part or assembly name are never replaced, so a character called like a mod cannot erase that mod's log tags. One-word names go through a single word-set scan and names with spaces through one alternation regex, so 2,000 names over 4 MB of log redact in about a second together with the rest of the redaction (offline measurement, 2026-10-10). Names typed in chat or written by other mods' logs in other shapes, and clan and server names, stay; README.txt says so.

**Per-session logs.** BepInEx rewrites `LogOutput.log` at every start (`[Logging.Disk] AppendLog = false` by default), so the preloader patcher keeps its own copy of each session: `BepInEx/progenitor-logs/session.log` (this start), `session-1.log` and `session-2.log`. It starts with `Progenitor session log · started <UTC> · <process> (pid N)`, carries BepInEx's default disk levels (Fatal, Error, Warning, Message, Info), follows BepInEx's `Unity` source blacklist, flushes every second and at once on errors, and stops at 50 MB with one `[truncated: ...]` line. A second game process of the same install that finds `session.log` open rotates nothing and writes `session-pid<pid>.log` (the three newest are kept); so does a start whose rotation could not move the previous `session.log` away (a viewer holding an older file), so a session log is never overwritten. If a BepInEx build lacks the disk-log blacklist or the preloader buffering listener, the listener stops consulting them instead of failing every line. This runs on the client, the hosted private game and the dedicated server wherever `Progenitor.Preloader.dll` is in `BepInEx/patchers`. `tools/package.ps1` puts it in the Thunderstore zip and asserts it is there; `tools/deploy.ps1` deploys it on request. Without it only the current `LogOutput.log` is collected. Progenitor's self-update does not replace it (it reads only Bootstrap, Core and the tables), so a new preloader arrives with a mod-manager update.

**Dedicated servers on this PC.** On the client, the worker thread lists every running `VRisingServer.exe` other than this process by its full image path (`QueryFullProcessImageName`, else `MainModule`; an unreadable one is counted in a note) and, for each install folder once, collects its `BepInEx/LogOutput.log` (and recent rolled ones), `ErrorLog.log`, `progenitor-logs/session*.log` and the Progenitor audit logs under `local-server-<n>/bepinex/`, plus the Unity log of its `-logFile` under `local-server-<n>/unity/` when its command line can be read (`ServerInstall.TryReadProcessCommandLine`, read-only access). The folder already collected as `hosted-server/` and this process's own folder are skipped, folders without `BepInEx` are skipped, and at most three servers are collected. Nothing in those folders is written. summary.txt names each by folder name only (`local-server-1: folder "<name>", this client is connected to it, -logFile collected`); connected means this client is in a world and its last join's port equals the server's game port (its command line, then its `ServerHostSettings.json` layers, else 9876) at a loopback or own address, and `connection unknown` is written when no join is recorded.

**Threading.** The click reads the game's facts on the main thread, builds the zip on a worker thread and posts the result back through `MainThread.Post` (Explorer and the clipboard run there). A second click while collecting fails with `Already collecting logs.` Offline measurement: three whole 6 MB logs with 9,000 SteamIDs and 250 addresses built a 1.2 MB zip in 1.1 s (`SupportBundleTests`, 2026-10-10).

**Bundle name (2026-10-10).** `vrising-mod-logs-<side>-<yyyyMMdd-HHmmss>.zip`: the bundle helps whichever mod is misbehaving, so its name and README are mod-agnostic (README.txt opens `V Rising mod support bundle (<side>)` and says once `Collected by Progenitor (Get help > Collect logs).`). Retention, **Last bundle** and the temp-file cleanup also recognize bundles of the old name `progenitor-logs-*`: one listing of each folder filtered by `SupportBundle.IsBundleName`; the command stays `.progenitor logs`.

**Logs saved dialog (SUPPORTDISCORD1, 2026-10-10).** After a save from any client entry point (the Mods tab's Collect logs tile, Get help's button, the title screen's tile), Explorer opens with the zip selected and its path goes on the clipboard as before, and then a framework dialog in the game's popup look (`UiDialogs.Choose`, `SupportOfferDialog`) asks:

- Title `Logs saved`; body `<file> (<size>) is on your Desktop. Post it in the #technical-support forum of the V Rising Mod Community Discord so the mod's developer can help.` (`is in <folder name>` for a chosen folder, `is in BepInEx/progenitor-support` on a fallback).
- **Join the Discord** opens `SupportBundle.DiscordInviteUrl` = `https://vrisingmods.com/discord` (the community's invite redirect, Thunderstore's "Modding Discord" link). **Open #technical-support** opens `SupportBundle.TechSupportChannelUrl` (the forum channel's `https://discord.com/channels/…` link); it is shown only while that constant is a Discord channel link. **Not now** closes it, as do Escape and a controller's B. The two link captions are too wide for one row with Not now, so the kit draws them as its option list above the Not now row; controller navigation is the dialog kit's (NAV1). The bridge answers it with `mods_ui dialog_choose` (`Join the Discord`, `Open #technical-support`, `no`).
- A link opens only from its button, https only, through `Application.OpenURL` on the main thread, with one log line (`Support: opened <url>.`). Nothing opens by itself any more (the earlier automatic open after a save is gone).
- **Offer to open the Discord after saving** (`[Support] AskToOpenDiscord`, default on, under Get help) turns the dialog off; the status line still says where the zip is.
- No dialog on the dedicated server (`.progenitor logs`), after a failed collect, or while a world is loading or joining: a client world whose state is neither `Idle` (the title) nor `Running` (in game) skips it with a log line (`SupportActions.DialogBlockedFor`). A dialog that cannot be shown leaves the status line alone and logs why.
- Readback: `SupportActions.LastOffer` (`shown`, `off`, `skipped: …`); the dialog itself is `mods_ui status` → `dialog`.

Status: source-checked, built and game-free tested (`SupportLandingAndTileTests`: the buttons for each link, the preference off, no automatic open, the loading skip; `DialogTests`: the dialog's buttons and answers; `SupportNamesAndDiagnosisTests`: README.txt's links), 2026-10-10. Not live-verified: the dialog over the title screen and in a world, Explorer's window and the dialog together, controller navigation of this dialog, and the links opening in the browser.

**Title screen tile.** The title's bottom-left icon row (`MainMenuNewView.LinkButtonSet.LinksParentNode`) gets one more icon at its end: `progenitor-ui-core-logs-tile`, the row's own size and spacing (rect, anchors and `LayoutElement` copied from the first native icon), the native icons' slate blue (`TitleLogsTile.TileBlue`, rgb(33, 54, 73) as measured on the live title screen; user, 2026-10-10: no red, match the official icons) with a gold zip-file glyph drawn in code (`ZipGlyph`, 128 px, white tinted by the Image).
